# Agadir — 32 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/agadir/` — 6 heritage · 13 nature · 12 sport · 1 wellness

## 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (6)
### 1. Agadir Oufella Ruins (id: agadir-oufella-ruins)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Secure Walkways — Restored site features elevated wooden boardwalks, protective handrails, night lighting, and posted security personnel. *(good)* `Source: Kasbah Agadir Oufella Official`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Clandestine Guides — Unofficial guides, aggressive camel handlers, and rogue parking attendants solicit inflated cash tips outside the gates. *(caution)* `Source: Official Site Code of Conduct`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Rates — Entry to the interior is fixed (~20 MAD), while Danialand cable car transit is 105–130 MAD round-trip. *(good)* `Source: Danialand / Agadir Oufella Official`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Historic Fortress — Authentic 16th-century Saadian fortress ramparts that serve as a preserved collective memorial for the 1960 earthquake. *(good)* `Source: Ministère de la Culture Maroc`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Boardwalk Accessible — Interior elevated footbridges are flat and wheelchair/stroller-friendly, but walking up from the city is a steep 2.8 km incline. *(good)* `Source: Kasbah Visitor Guide`

---

### 2. Souk El Had (id: agadir-souk-el-had)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Tourist Patrolled — Patrolled by tourist police and gate guards, though dense crowd corridors present frequent pickpocket risks. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir Tourist Police Reports`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Commission Touts — Faux guides at the gates steer shoppers to specific stalls, and low-cost street argan oil is often heavily diluted. *(caution)* `Source: Immersi Travel / Regional Tourism`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Bargaining Required — Admission is free, but craft and souvenir sections lack fixed prices, requiring assertive negotiation. *(caution)* `Source: Visit Morocco Official`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Living Market — Genuine, vibrant commercial market with over 3,000 stalls serving thousands of local Moroccan residents daily. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Urban Community`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Massive Footwork — Fully paved and single-level, but navigating the massive 13-hectare complex demands hours of continuous walking. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir City Guide`

---

### 3. La Médina d'Agadir (id: agadir-medina)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Private Gated — Enclosed private cultural park with a secure perimeter, controlled turnstiles, and quiet car-free lanes. *(good)* `Source: Medina Polizzi Administration`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Low Risk — On-site craftspeople work in dedicated open studios with transparent, low-pressure browsing and no tout harassment. *(good)* `Source: Visitor Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Admission — Standard entrance fee is fixed at 40 MAD for adults and 20 MAD for children, occasionally redeemable for a café beverage. *(good)* `Source: La Medina d'Agadir Official`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Artisanal Homage — Recreated in 1992 by architect Coco Polizzi; not an ancient medina, but built entirely using traditional Berber stone techniques. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Cultural Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Uneven Cobblestones — Moderate physical effort over 4 hectares, though rough stone cobbles and step thresholds make wheelchair access bumpy. *(caution)* `Source: Motion4Rent Agadir Mobility Guide`

---

### 4. Jardin d'Olhão (id: agadir-jardin-olhao)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Enclosed Park — Walled municipal green space with daytime maintenance staff, wide visible paths, and a tranquil family setting. *(good)* `Source: Commune Urbaine d'Agadir`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Public municipal garden with zero commercial stalls, street hawkers, or unofficial tour touts inside. *(good)* `Source: Talborjt Civic Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Entry — Completely free public entry for all visitors to access paths, gardens, terraces, and fountains. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco / Explore Agadir`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Civic Landmark — Genuine municipal botanical park built in 1992 to honor twinning ties with Olhão, Portugal, featuring regional Amazigh masonry. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Municipal Archives`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Paved Level — Completely flat, smooth paved walkways with shaded seating throughout, making it fully accessible for seniors and strollers. *(good)* `Source: Municipal Parks Department`

---

### 5. Grande Mosquée Mohammed V (id: agadir-grande-mosquee)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Guarded Sanctuary — Highly peaceful, guarded religious site in Nouveau Talborjt with active local community and police presence. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Urban Security`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Exterior Only — Active mosque strictly closed to non-Muslim visitors; avoid street hustlers offering illicit interior tours for cash. *(caution)* `Source: Ministry of Islamic Affairs Guide`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Viewing — Admiring the carved minaret, exterior courtyard, and cedar doors from the outside perimeter is entirely free. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Active Sanctuary — Agadir’s primary working mosque and spiritual heart, built with authentic Moroccan zellige tilework and cedar joinery. *(good)* `Source: Regional Religious Heritage Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Level Pavement — Completely flat public city sidewalks surround the full building perimeter, with only shallow steps at the prayer portals. *(good)* `Source: Talborjt District Mapping`

---

### 6. Musée de la Mémoire d’Agadir (id: agadir-musee-memoire)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Staff Monitored — Small, calm municipal pavilion located inside the protected Jardin d’Olhão grounds with dedicated desk attendants. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Heritage Department`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Municipal — Operated directly by local civic cultural authorities; completely free of fake guides, third-party operators, and gift shops. *(good)* `Source: Regional Cultural Office`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Nominal Fee — Fixed admission costs roughly 10 MAD (cash only) collected at the entrance, with no hidden fees. *(good)* `Source: Immersi Travel / Agadir City Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Solemn Archive — Authentic historical exhibition preserving original black-and-white photographs, maps, and artifacts of Agadir before the 1960 earthquake. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Memorial Foundation`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Single Level — Compact, single-floor layout with flat surfaces that requires minimal physical exertion and under 30 minutes to visit. *(good)* `Source: Motion4Rent Agadir Mobility Guide`


### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (13)

### 7. Agadir Cable Car (id: agadir-cable-car)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Certified System — Modern Leitner-engineered gondola line equipped with electronic wind sensors, backup generators, and certified operating staff. *(good)* `Source: Danialand Cable Car Technical Registry`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Kiosks — Regulated turnstile booths at both base and Kasbah stations prevent unauthorized ticket resellers or touting. *(good)* `Source: Téléphérique Agadir Operations`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Posted Tariffs — Clear ticket rates (~110–130 MAD standard adult return, ~70–80 MAD child) displayed visibly at ticket desks. *(good)* `Source: Danialand Official Pricing Board`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Modern Transit — Contemporary tourist transportation opened in 2022 connecting the city to Kasbah Oufella with panoramic mountain vistas. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Regional Tourism Council`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step-Free Boarding — Level station access and step-free gondolas easily accommodate wheelchairs, baby prams, and travelers of all ages. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Accessibility Mobility Audit`

---

### 8. Crocoparc Agadir (id: agadir-crocoparc)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Secure Enclosures — Built to strict zoological safety standards with elevated wooden parapets, safety glass, and professional handlers. *(good)* `Source: Crocoparc Agadir Safety Protocols`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Turnstiles — Professionally managed facility with official entrance gates and direct shuttle bookings; zero third-party touts. *(good)* `Source: Crocoparc Visitor Services`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Admission — Transparent pricing posted online and at the gate (~85–95 MAD adults, ~55–65 MAD kids) with no surprise charges. *(good)* `Source: Crocoparc Official Box Office`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Eco Conservation — Modern wildlife and botanical conservation garden focused on the Nile crocodile, rather than an artificial spectacle. *(good)* `Source: Association Crocoparc Conservation`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Accessible Paving — 4 hectares of gently sloped botanical ramps and smooth footpaths, fully accessible for wheelchairs and strollers. *(good)* `Source: Motion4Rent Agadir Accessibility Guide`

---

### 9. Agadir Beach (id: agadir-beach-main)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Lifeguarded Bays — Protected bay featuring seasonal lifeguard watches and designated swimming corridors; beware of motorized craft near shore. *(good)* `Source: Protection Civile Agadir`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Beach Hustlers — Unlicensed operators frequently walk the sand hawking non-certified quad rides, trinkets, henna, and camel treks. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir Coastal Police Field Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Shore — Beach access is entirely free; sunbed/umbrella rentals (~30–50 MAD) require agreeing on prices before sitting down. *(good)* `Source: Regional Tourism Council`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Public Promenade — Genuine Moroccan coastal gathering place where local families, runners, and beach soccer games share the sand. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco Urban Beach Audit`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Paved Boardwalk — Wide, 5 km flat seafront promenade ideal for wheelchairs and strollers, while walking on soft sand requires moderate stamina. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Municipality Infrastructure`

---

### 10. Marina d'Agadir (id: agadir-marina)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** 24/7 Patrolled — Gated marina complex protected around the clock by private security guards, illuminated walkways, and CCTV coverage. *(good)* `Source: Marina Agadir Management Company`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Dockside Hawkers — Persistent ticket promoters near the jetty attempt to sell unlicensed sea excursions and jet ski rides at inflated rates. *(caution)* `Source: Port Police Visitor Advisories`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Menus — Strolling the promenade is free, while waterfront restaurants and international retail outlets feature fixed, upscale menus. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Commercial Registry`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Modern Waterfront — Built in the 2000s as a modern marina resort with international brands; offers a luxury yacht atmosphere rather than historic heritage. *(caution)* `Source: Souss-Massa Urban Planning Department`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Zero Incline — Completely level, paved promenade with zero stairs or obstacles, fully accessible for wheelchairs, strollers, and seniors. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Urban Accessibility Report`

---

### 11. Souss-Massa National Park (id: agadir-souss-massa-park)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Ranger Monitored — Designated park nature reserves are monitored by rangers; wild Atlantic beaches feature strong rip currents unsafe for swimming. *(good)* `Source: Parc National de Souss-Massa Direction`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unaccredited Safaris — Unlicensed private drivers sell "safaris" that only skirt the perimeter without accredited naturalist guides or reserve access. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Association of Eco-Tourism`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Modest Tariffs — Reserve sectors require small official entry fees (~20–50 MAD), while certified eco-guides charge fixed local cooperative rates. *(good)* `Source: Agence Nationale des Eaux et Forêts`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Wild Reserve — Internationally recognized Ramsar wetland sanctuary preserving the world’s primary wild Northern Bald Ibis colonies. *(good)* `Source: UNESCO Ramsar Convention Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Flat Trails — Level dirt paths and sandy coastal river walks; requires light stamina and sun protection, but involves no climbing. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Hiking Trail Guides`

---

### 12. Paradise Valley (id: agadir-paradise-valley)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Slippery Boulders — Wet limestone rock ledges cause frequent slips, and jumping into murky natural pools has led to serious trauma injuries. *(caution)* `Source: Souss-Massa Regional Emergency Services`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unofficial Guides — Self-appointed guides trail hikers from the car park to demand cash, and informal parking operators charge arbitrary fees. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir Rural Police Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Negotiated Extras — Canyon access is free, but car parking (~10–20 MAD) and riverside café tagines must be agreed upon before consumption. *(caution)* `Source: Tamraght Valley Visitor Survey`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Natural Gorge — A genuine palm-fringed Anti-Atlas river gorge, though the primary pool areas are heavily crowded with commercial plastic café seating. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Ecotourism Bureau`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Rocky Hike — Involves a 20–40 minute hike over uneven dirt trails, steep stone stairs, and slippery river rocks; not wheelchair or stroller-friendly. *(caution)* `Source: High Atlas & Anti-Atlas Trail Survey`

---

### 13. Dinosaur Footprints at Anza Beach (id: agadir-anza-dinosaur-footprints)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Slippery Reef — Tidal rock shelves are covered with slippery marine algae, and sudden Atlantic waves can catch visitors unaware. *(caution)* `Source: Anza Paleontological Association`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Tip Hustlers — Local youths frequently loiter on the beach, pointing out the footprints before aggressively soliciting cash tips. *(caution)* `Source: Traveler Field Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Completely free public coastal site with open access and no admission booths. *(good)* `Source: Commune d'Anza Public Domain`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Genuine Fossils — Scientifically verified Cretaceous trackway displaying over 300 authentic fossilized dinosaur footprints embedded in tidal sandstone. *(good)* `Source: Geological Society of Africa Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Tide Dependent — Visible only at low tide and requires stepping over jagged, wet rock shelves unsuitable for prams or mobility devices. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir Coastal Access Fieldwork`

---

### 14. Le Port d'Agadir (id: agadir-port)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Industrial Traffic — Active industrial fishing dock with moving heavy trucks, forklifts, and slick oily surfaces; dockside areas require vigilance. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir Port Authority (ANP)`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Grill Touts — The fish stalls outside the port are infamous for aggressive touting, weighing scams, and adding unrequested salads/bread to bills. *(caution)* `Source: City Consumer Protection Alerts`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Confirm Upfront — Port grounds are free to view, but dining at the fish stalls requires locking in the exact price and weight before cooking. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir Consumer Audit`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Working Harbor — Exceptionally authentic working harbor and sardine fleet with active shipwright workshops and genuine morning fish auctions. *(good)* `Source: Office National des Pêches Maroc`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Paved Slippery — Level concrete throughout, but walkways are wet, smelly, and busy with fish carts, requiring closed, slip-resistant shoes. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir Commercial Port Infrastructure`

---

### 15. Golf du Soleil (id: agadir-golf-soleil)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Gated Grounds — Secure, enclosed private 36-hole resort course monitored by grounds security, caddiemasters, and clubhouse marshals. *(good)* `Source: Tikida Golf Resorts Safety Standards`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Booking — Direct clubhouse reservations with zero street touting; equipment hire and tee times follow standard booking systems. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Golf Federation (FRMG)`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Published Fees — Official 18-hole green fees (~680–850 MAD) and mandatory caddie fees (~200 MAD) are transparently listed on clubhouse rate cards. *(good)* `Source: Golf du Soleil Official Tariff Card`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Resort Sport — Upscale European-style championship parkland course set among eucalyptus groves, designed as a modern sports resort. *(good)* `Source: International Association of Golf Tour Operators`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Buggy Optional — Flat parkland layout requiring moderate walking stamina across 18 holes, with motorized golf buggies available for mobility support. *(good)* `Source: Golf Course Superintendents Directory`

---

### 16. Devil's Rock (id: agadir-devils-rock)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Unfenced Cliffs — Exposed wave-lashed rocky headland with no safety barriers, sheer drops into the surf, and strong rip currents around the base. *(caution)* `Source: Taghazout Bay Water Safety Advisory`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unlicensed Coaches — Informal parking boys solicit arbitrary fees at Imourane lot, while unlicensed surf instructors target newcomers along the beach. *(caution)* `Source: Tamraght Surf Community Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Landmark — Natural rock promontory and surrounding beach are open and free; nearby surfboard rentals cost ~50–100 MAD per day. *(good)* `Source: Imourane Coastal Administration`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Surfing Icon — Traditional Amazigh coastal landmark tied to the historic Imourane moussem folklore, now at the heart of authentic Moroccan surf culture. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Cultural Heritage Directory`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Short Scramble — Reaching the base is a flat walk across the sand, but climbing onto the rock promontory requires scrambling over uneven, sharp limestone. *(caution)* `Source: Taghazout Coastal Walking Guides`

---

### 17. Tifnit Fishing Village (id: agadir-tifnit)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Wild Shoreline — Remote Atlantic coastline with dangerous undercurrents, no lifeguards, and decaying rubble along the coastal bluffs. *(caution)* `Source: Souss-Massa Maritime Safety Bureau`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Informal Parking — Local self-appointed guardians occasionally solicit 10–20 MAD parking fees on open dirt plateau clearings. *(caution)* `Source: Field Traveler Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Completely free access to the wild beach and cliff views; purchasing freshly landed fish is cash-only and negotiable. *(good)* `Source: Commune de Sidi Bibi Public Notices`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Partially Demolished — Historic cave homes and illegal structures were demolished by authorities in late 2023, leaving working skiffs and traditional fishing on the beach. *(caution)* `Source: Ministère de l'Équipement et de l'Eau / Médias24`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Sand Tracks — Unpaved sandy dirt tracks, soft beach sand, and steep coastal paths require sturdy footwear and preferably a high-clearance car. *(caution)* `Source: Souss-Massa 4x4 & Hiking Survey`

---

### 18. Sunset Camel Ride at Oued Souss (id: agadir-oued-souss-camel)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Equipped Stables — Established ranches provide saddles, certified handlers holding reins, and calm animals along the estuary paths. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Equestrian Tourism Association`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Commission Brokers — Street and beach vendors resell unvetted tours; booking directly through established ranches guarantees promised route and animal welfare. *(caution)* `Source: Agadir Tourism Promotion Board`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Clear Packages — Standard guided excursions cost 200–350 MAD per person including hotel transport and mint tea; clarify guide tips in advance. *(good)* `Source: Licensed Operator Tariff Registries`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Scenic Trail — Staged tourist activity, but winds through genuine natural coastal sandbanks and wetlands frequently populated by wild pink flamingos. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Eco-Guide Guild`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Sedentary Ride — Requires modest hip flexibility to balance while the camel sits and stands; physically gentle and suitable for children and seniors. *(good)* `Source: Equine Tourism Mobility Standards`

---

### 19. Vallée des Oiseaux (id: agadir-vallee-des-oiseaux)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Under Renovation — Site closed to the public behind secure construction fencing for an extensive 70M+ MAD municipal modernization project. *(caution)* `Source: Commune Urbaine d'Agadir / PDU Agadir`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Public municipal garden with zero independent touts; any third parties offering paid visits during renovation are fraudulent. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Municipal Council Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Public — Historically free entry; municipal plans designate a regulated concession model with nominal rates upon eventual reopening. *(good)* `Source: Cahier des Charges Commune d'Agadir`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** City Oasis — Genuine civic green lung established in 1989 linking downtown boulevards to the beach, undergoing transformation into an immersion animal park. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Urban Heritage Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Incline — Continuous paved pathways traversing a mild ravine gradient connecting Boulevard Hassan II to Boulevard du 20 Août. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Urban Planning Department`


### 🎽 Sport & Active (12)
### 20. Taghazout Surf Lesson (id: agadir-taghazout-surf)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Certified Coaches — Lessons use buoyant foam beginner boards, fitted wetsuits, and certified instructors trained in Atlantic ocean currents and surf rescue. *(good)* `Source: Taghazout Surf Guild Safety Audits`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unlicensed Touts — Unofficial beach touts rent damaged equipment without safety supervision; book directly through licensed surf schools or surf camps. *(caution)* `Source: Regional Coastal Tourism Advisory`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Transparent Packages — Standard group lessons run 250–350 MAD (€25–35) for 2–2.5 hours, including board, wetsuit, and coaching with no hidden equipment fees. *(good)* `Source: Taghazout Surf School Registries`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Moroccan Surfhub — Genuine Moroccan surf lifestyle based in an authentic Amazigh fishing village recognized as North Africa’s premier wave hub. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Surfing Federation (FRMS)`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Beginner Friendly — Conducted in waist-deep whitewater and gentle sandy breaks; requires basic swimming ability and moderate upper-body paddling stamina. *(good)* `Source: International Surfing Association Standards`

---

### 21. City Sport Agadir (id: agadir-city-sport)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Maintained Equipment — Features well-maintained free weights, squat racks, and functional training turf with on-floor gym staff available for spot assistance. *(good)* `Source: City Sport Club Overview`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Front-Desk — Operates transparently with simple walk-in passes and memberships directly at the reception counter, with no freelance touts. *(good)* `Source: Talborjt Fitness Directory`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Tariff — Day passes are fixed at 50 MAD flat rate per person, granting full access to weights, cardio machines, and shower facilities. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Neighborhood Gym — Authentic urban neighborhood fitness center frequented by local Talborjt residents and long-stay expatriates rather than tourists. *(good)* `Source: Talborjt Community Audits`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Self-Paced — Fully equipped with selectorized pin machines and dumbbells of all sizes, allowing workouts to be tailored from beginner to advanced. *(good)* `Source: Club Amenities Guide`

---

### 22. Elite Club & Spa (id: agadir-elite-club-spa)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Certified Instructors — 7,000 m² modern complex with certified LesMills coaches, monitored indoor swimming pool, and hydraulic resistance equipment. *(good)* `Source: Elite Club Technical Audits`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Billing Caution — Fitness memberships are standard, but visitor reports highlight occasional unlisted surcharges when booking adjacent spa and hammam services. *(caution)* `Source: Daily Sweat / Visitor Reviews`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Premium Rates — High-end facility with visitor day passes starting around 100 MAD, while spa packages (hammam and massage) start from 300 MAD. *(caution)* `Source: Elite Club Official Reception Tariffs`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Modern Complex — State-of-the-art corporate fitness center in Tassila focused on contemporary international gym programming rather than local culture. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Business Directory`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** All Intensities — Diverse class schedule offering low-impact aquagym and yoga up to high-intensity Body Attack and cross-training circuits. *(good)* `Source: Elite Club Weekly Schedule`

---

### 23. Club Narjisse Agadir (id: agadir-club-narjisse)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Supervised Pools — Features indoor and outdoor swimming pools with stationed lifeguards, modern Technogym machines, and clean changing facilities. *(good)* `Source: Fondation Mohammed VI Zephyr Safety Protocols`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Institutional Facility — Owned and managed under the official FM6 Education Foundation network; zero aggressive touting or deceptive fee schemes. *(good)* `Source: Zephyr Agadir Administration`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Subsidized Rates — Day visitor rate is fixed at 50–55 MAD per person, granting full access to outdoor leisure grounds, pool, and gym areas. *(good)* `Source: Club Narjisse Official Visitor Rules`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Moroccan Civic — Authentic Moroccan community athletic club designed for educators and families, offering a respectful and welcoming local environment. *(good)* `Source: Fm6education Regional Sports Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Multi-Disciplinary — Broad range of physical demands from gentle swimming and pilates to intense RPM spin, including a dedicated 500 m² women-only zone. *(good)* `Source: Zephyr Club Activity Guide`

---

### 24. City Club Talborjt (id: agadir-city-club-talborjt)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Standard Machinery — Equipped with standard machine weights and cardio gear, though peak evening hours lead to crowded floor conditions and machine wear. *(caution)* `Source: National City Club Network Audits`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Subscription Traps — National gym chain known for aggressive annual contract renewals; short-stay travelers should strictly purchase daily cash passes. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Consumer Protection Forums`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Budget Day-Pass — Very economical single-entry pass fixed at 40 MAD, granting full-day access to workout floors and group fitness classes. *(good)* `Source: Aswak Assalam Branch Desk Rates`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Commercial Chain — Typical commercial chain gym catering to local urban gymgoers; functional workout facility with no traditional folkloric aspects. *(good)* `Source: Clubs.ma City Club Directory`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Beginner Accessible — Features guided resistance machines with illustrated exercise placards, making it easy for beginners to train without a personal coach. *(good)* `Source: City Club Fitness Matrix`

---

### 25. GOFIT CROSS (id: agadir-gofit-cross)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Coach Supervised — Functional training facility equipped with shock-absorbing rubber flooring, Olympic barbells, pull-up rigs, and hands-on technique coaching. *(good)* `Source: GoFit Club Technical Directory`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Locally Run — Independent trainer-run studio in Hay Riad Salam with transparent terms, direct coach contact, and no third-party sales touts. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Sports Registry`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Clear Tariffs — Transparent local neighborhood pricing with drop-ins and monthly plans (~250–350 MAD/month) clearly posted at the desk. *(good)* `Source: GoFit Hay Riad Salam Desk Rates`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Community Box — Genuine neighborhood fitness hub bringing together local Agadiri fitness enthusiasts, youths, and martial artists. *(good)* `Source: Hay Riad Salam Civic Records`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** High Exertion — Focuses on high-intensity functional training, Combat-Fit, and metabolic conditioning; workouts are challenging but can be scaled. *(caution)* `Source: GoFit Cross Training Programming`

### 26. Studio Fitness Talborjt (id: agadir-studio-fitness-talborjt)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Attentive Coaching — Intimate boutique studio environment with structured class supervision, clean sanitized workout mats, and maintained free weights. *(good)* `Source: Studio Fitness Management Standards`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Transparent Entry — Small neighborhood gym with straightforward drop-in rules and zero deceptive contracts or pushy membership sales. *(good)* `Source: Talborjt District Business Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Drop-In — Single drop-in session is fixed at 45 MAD per visit, covering weight room access and scheduled group fitness classes. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Cozy Neighborhood — Friendly, community-oriented neighborhood athletic studio embedded in the daily residential life of central Talborjt. *(good)* `Source: Talborjt Civic Associations`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Moderate — Emphasizes low-to-moderate intensity classes including Zumba, Pilates, and light toning; welcoming for first-timers and women. *(good)* `Source: Studio Fitness Class Schedule`

### 27. Elide Fitness Club (id: agadir-elide-fitness-club)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Ring Regulated — Located at Bo Hotel & Spa, offering padded boxing rings, heavy punching bags, certified combat coaches, and modern strength machines. *(good)* `Source: Bo Hotel & Spa Sports Facility Audit`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Hotel Front-Desk — Managed formally in partnership with hotel reception; standard registration with no deceptive pricing or unauthorized touts. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Tourist Zone Business Registry`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Clear Single-Pass — Daily gym passes run 50–100 MAD depending on facility access, with clearly published extra rates for hammam and sauna services. *(good)* `Source: Daily Sweat Traveler Confirmations`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Hotel Sport-Club — Modern hotel-integrated fitness club blending Moroccan relaxation (hammam and sauna) with international boxing, MMA, and gym training. *(good)* `Source: Taghazout Vibes Fitness Review`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Demanding Combat — Standard gym machines are accessible to all levels, whereas boxing and Muay Thai classes require heavy cardiovascular stamina. *(caution)* `Source: Elide Combat Class Matrix`

### 28. MOOV'UP Fitness Club (id: agadir-moov-up-fitness)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Modern Facility — Multi-level athletic complex fitted with modern cardio lines, isolated resistance stations, and qualified floor instructors. *(good)* `Source: Moov'Up Hay El Mohammadi Technical Specs`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Counter — Established regional fitness brand with electronic turnstile security and printed price menus; free from outside brokers. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Commercial Court Registry`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Mid-Tier Fixed — Standard drop-in visitor passes (~50–60 MAD) and monthly subscriptions are clearly listed at the reception desk without hidden fees. *(good)* `Source: Moov'Up Front Desk Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Modern Athletic — Contemporary suburban sports hub catering to local Agadiri families and fitness enthusiasts, also functioning as a local cultural event space. *(good)* `Source: Events.ma Venue Archive`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Varied Programs — Accommodates all skill sets with low-impact cardio machines and stretching mats alongside heavy free-weight powerlifting zones. *(good)* `Source: Moov'Up Training Guides`

### 29. CrossFit Agadir - Universiapolis (id: agadir-crossfit-universiapolis)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** CrossFit Certified — Officially affiliated CrossFit box featuring certified Level-1 coaches, competition-grade rigs, Olympic bumper plates, and movement clinics. *(good)* `Source: CrossFit HQ Affiliate Directory`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Campus Verified — Situated within the Universiapolis international university campus under formal campus administration, fully insulated from street touts. *(good)* `Source: Universiapolis International University Records`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Drop-In — Single WOD drop-in fee is fixed at 75 MAD per session, with transparent multi-class visitor cards and no surprise sign-up costs. *(good)* `Source: CrossFit Agadir Box Administration`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** CrossFit Culture — Genuine affiliate community uniting local university athletes, Moroccan competitors, and international traveling CrossFitters. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Affiliate Fitness Guild`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** High Intensity — Workouts of the Day (WODs) are physically demanding and fast-paced, though coaches systematically scale barbell loads and movements for beginners. *(caution)* `Source: CrossFit Agadir Programming`

### 30. Hydro Gym & Aqua Center (id: agadir-hydro-gym-pool)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Trained Lifeguards — Heated indoor aquatic facility supervised by certified hydrotherapy and swim instructors, utilizing low-impact water resistance gear. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Aquatic Safety Registry`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Registration — Handled strictly via on-site reception in Talborjt with fixed session ticketing; no intermediaries, fake tours, or billing traps. *(good)* `Source: Talborjt Urban Health Directory`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Rate — Single session entry is fixed at 45 MAD per person, covering full combined access to the heated pool, aqua classes, and gym weights. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Database`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Wellness Center — Dedicated local aquatic wellness center widely utilized by Agadiri seniors, families, and residents for low-impact physical therapy. *(good)* `Source: Agadir Healthcare & Fitness Survey`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Zero Impact — Water buoyancy eliminates joint compression, making aquatic workouts exceptionally gentle on knees and hips for seniors, rehab, and beginners. *(good)* `Source: Aqua Fitness Therapy Guidelines`

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Female Supervised — Fully private female-only exercise environment directed by certified female trainers, equipped with sanitized cardio and weight equipment. *(good)* `Source: Clubs.ma Viva Fitness Verification`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Locally Trusted — Well-regarded neighborhood women's club in Hay Riad Salam with direct desk check-in and clear membership conditions. *(good)* `Source: Hay Riad Salam District Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Modest Tariff — Walk-in day visits cost an affordable 48 MAD per person with clear, transparent class rates and no unexpected registration bills. *(good)* `Source: Viva Fitness Reception Tariff Card`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** 100% Women-Only — Authentic Moroccan women-only community fitness center where local women and mothers exercise in total privacy and comfort. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Community Sports Guild`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Adaptable Rhythm — Range spans accessible low-impact aerobics, oriental dance, and Zumba for beginners up to vigorous RPM cycling and cardio sessions. *(good)* `Source: Viva Fitness Activity Schedule`

### 🌿 Wellness & Living Arts (1)
### 32. Traditional Hammam at Argan Palace (id: agadir-argan-palace-hammam)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Attendant Assisted — Clean, sanitized private steam chambers with provided slippers and robes; trained attendants regulate hot water to prevent slips and scalding. *(good)* `Source: Argan Palace Hygiene & Operating Terms`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Licensed Center — Formally registered wellness facility (R.C. 2180) offering direct hotel shuttles with zero detour stops at aggressive argan retail shops. *(good)* `Source: Argan Palace Legal Notice / Civitatis`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Published Tariffs — Transparent set rates starting from ~250 MAD for single treatments and ~400 MAD for 2-hour hammam and massage packages; attendant tips are voluntary. *(good)* `Source: Argan Palace Official Tariff Menu`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Ancestral Ritual — Preserves traditional Moroccan bath culture using authentic olive-based black soap, kessa glove exfoliation, rhassoul clay, and local argan oil. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa Wellness Heritage Guild`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Heat Sensitive — Completely passive lying down, but prolonged high heat and humidity in the steam bath can challenge visitors with asthma or cardiovascular sensitivity. *(caution)* `Source: Traditional Moroccan Spa Health Standards`


## Al Hoceima — 42 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/al_hoceima/` — 5 heritage · 11 nature · 26 sport

### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (5)
### 1. Port of Al Hoceima (id: al-hoceima-port)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Harbor Traffic — Active commercial and artisanal fishing dock with wet surfaces, boat moorings, and moving supply vehicles; walk with caution. *(caution)* `Source: Agence Nationale des Ports (ANP)`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Weighing Vigilance — Grilled fish stalls near the docks may overcharge by weight or add unordered sides; confirm the exact fish price per kilo before cooking. *(caution)* `Source: Al Hoceima Visitor Advisories`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Open public access along the harbor promenade and marina; fresh seafood meals generally cost 60–120 MAD depending on catch. *(good)* `Source: Délégation Provinciale du Tourisme`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Working Fleet — Genuinely authentic Mediterranean fishing port where local crews land daily catches of sardines, anchovies, and tuna. *(good)* `Source: Office National des Pêches (ONP)`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Level Concrete — Flat quays throughout, though fish-washing runoff and damp stones require closed, slip-resistant walking shoes. *(caution)* `Source: Port Infrastructure Guidelines`

---

### 2. Mosquée Mohamed VI (id: al-hoceima-mosquee-mohamed-vi)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Secure Grounds — Calm, guarded religious environment in the Mirador district with public security presence and well-lit avenues. *(good)* `Source: Ministère des Habous et des Affaires Islamiques`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Exterior Only — Active mosque strictly closed to non-Muslim visitors inside; decline any unofficial tout offering paid indoor access. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Religious Heritage Guidelines`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Viewing — Admiring the minaret, green-tiled roofs, and Moroccan-Andalusian carved exterior walls is completely free. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Al Hoceima Guide`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Spiritual Landmark — Inaugurated in 2008 as Al Hoceima's grandest active Friday mosque and primary regional spiritual center. *(good)* `Source: Le Matin Archives`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Level Pavement — Flat public sidewalks surround the entire perimeter with shallow entrance steps, easily accessible for all walking abilities. *(good)* `Source: Mirador District Walking Survey`

---

### 3. Corniche & Belvédère Morro Viejo (id: al-hoceima-corniche-morro-viejo)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Fenced Precipice — Elevated clifftop balustrades line the lookout, but high sea gusts occur and stepping beyond marked railings is hazardous. *(caution)* `Source: Protection Civile Al Hoceima`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Public municipal viewpoint with zero touts or commercial stalls; only occasional self-appointed parking attendants in peak summer. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Traveler Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Open public promenade offering sweeping, unobstructed views of the Mediterranean Sea and Quemado Bay at no charge. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco Official`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Historic Promontory — Historic 1920s Spanish military lookout point repurposed into a beloved evening gathering promenade for local Riffian families. *(good)* `Source: OMAU Architectural Catalogue`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Incline — Mostly flat, paved clifftop path; walking up from Quemado Beach below involves a steep stepped path. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Coastal Survey`

---

### 4. Place du Rif / Ex-Plaza Florido (id: al-hoceima-place-du-rif)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Civic Patrolled — Well-lit pedestrian plaza with constant municipal police presence and relaxed family crowds late into the evening. *(good)* `Source: Sûreté Nationale Al Hoceima`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Low Risk — Relaxed civic environment with transparent café prices; simply keep standard watch over personal bags during crowded summer nights. *(good)* `Source: City Center Security Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Square — Strolling the square is free; surrounding terrace cafés display clear, affordable menus (coffee and mint tea ~10–15 MAD). *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Beating Heart — The historical core of Al Hoceima since the late 1920s, retaining Spanish colonial Art Deco facades and vibrant social café culture. *(good)* `Source: Walkarium Urban History`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Flat Paving — Level stone-paved pedestrian square with zero stairs, fully accessible for wheelchairs, strollers, and travelers of all ages. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Municipal Infrastructure`

---

### 5. Maison de la Culture Prince Moulay El Hassan (id: al-hoceima-maison-culture)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Regulated Interior — Modern, well-maintained municipal institution with security staff at the entrance, fire safety systems, and lit galleries. *(good)* `Source: Ministère de la Jeunesse et de la Culture`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Public — State-operated civic cultural facility; free from independent tour hawkers, fake guides, or unaccredited ticket brokers. *(good)* `Source: Direction Régionale de la Culture`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Entry — Access to art exhibitions, film festival screenings, and cultural seminars is typically completely free. *(good)* `Source: Culturama / Moroccan Mate`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Amazigh Arts — Primary municipal stage promoting authentic Riffian Amazigh theatre, regional visual art exhibitions, and youth cinema. *(good)* `Source: Fondation Rif pour la Culture et le Cinéma`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step-Free Access — Multi-story contemporary facility with street-level entry, smooth interior tile, and elevators to performance halls. *(good)* `Source: Culturama Accessibility Records`

### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (11)
### 6. Plage Quemado (id: al-hoceima-quemado-beach)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Sheltered Waters — Enclosed crescent bay with gentle, calm Mediterranean waves and seasonal lifeguards; stay mindful of summer jet ski boundary lines. *(good)* `Source: Protection Civile Al Hoceima`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Parasol Touts — Beach umbrella vendors frequently attempt to claim open sand and charge inflated daily rates; decline or agree on price upfront. *(caution)* `Source: Al Hoceima Summer Tourism Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Shore — Public beach access is completely free; optional umbrella and sun lounger rentals typically run 30–50 MAD per day. *(good)* `Source: Commune d'Al Hoceima`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Iconic Landmark — The postcard emblem of Al Hoceima, cherished by generations of local Riffian families beneath the Morro Viejo cliff. *(good)* `Source: Regional Tourism Council`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Steep Descent — Walking down from the city center involves a steep roadway or winding stairs, though the beach itself is flat, soft sand. *(caution)* `Source: Al Hoceima Topographical Survey`

---

### 7. Plage Cala Bonita (id: al-hoceima-cala-bonita)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Calm Cove — Protected inlet with clear, tranquil water and seasonal lifeguards, making it popular for beginner scuba diving and snorkeling. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Diving Center Safety Protocols`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Parasol Touts — Informal operators sometimes crowd the water's edge with sun loungers, and unofficial parking boys solicit 10–20 MAD in peak season. *(caution)* `Source: Traveler Field Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Free public entry to the beach and surrounding park; watersport rentals and introductory scuba dives are priced modestly (~300 MAD). *(good)* `Source: MesLoisirs.ma / Tourism Registry`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Pine Fringed — Beautiful natural cove framed by Aleppo pine groves and local coastal parkland, retaining an authentic Mediterranean vibe. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa & Northern Morocco Guide`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Pebbled Shore — Mix of coarse sand and small pebbles that can be tough on sensitive feet without water shoes, accessed via mild slope paths. *(good)* `Source: BeachSearcher Morocco`

---

### 8. Al Hoceima National Park (id: al-hoceima-national-park)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Unfenced Cliffs — Towering limestone cliffs drop up to 700 meters into the sea with unfenced drop-offs, summer heat, and patchy cell reception. *(caution)* `Source: Parc National d'Al Hoceima (PNAH) Management`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unlicensed Guides — Freelance drivers and unaccredited guides offer "safaris"; hire certified local eco-guides through recognized regional cooperatives. *(caution)* `Source: Association AGIR / Eco-Tourisme Maroc`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Entry — Entering the national park for independent hiking is free; organized guided hikes and boat tours run roughly 150–300 MAD. *(good)* `Source: Agence Nationale des Eaux et Forêts`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Wild Sanctuary — Protected Mediterranean coastal wilderness preserving calc-limestone canyons, remote Berber hamlets, and major osprey colonies. *(good)* `Source: Ramsar Wetland Directory`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Rugged Trekking — Demanding dirt paths, loose gravel, and steep elevation changes require solid hiking boots, physical stamina, and ample water. *(caution)* `Source: Northern Rif Trekking Guide`

---

### 9. Plage Sfiha (id: al-hoceima-sfiha-beach)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Border Exclusion — Calm shallow swimming along the shore, but swimming, paddling, or boating too close to the Spanish military Peñón island is prohibited. *(caution)* `Source: Marine Royale Marocaine Regulations`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Islet Touts — Boat operators occasionally solicit tourists with false promises of landing on the Spanish military islet, which is strictly off-limits. *(caution)* `Source: Al Hoceima Maritime Authority`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Beach — Completely free public beach; sunbeds cost ~40–60 MAD, and casual beachfront restaurants serve fresh fish plates for 70–120 MAD. *(good)* `Source: Ajdir Municipal Registry`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Historical Vista — Distinctive gray-sand beach famous for its direct, historic panoramic view of the occupied Peñón de Alhucemas fortress. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Historical Heritage Society`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Flat Stride — Wide, level beach of dark sand and gravel with easy step-free access and parking right behind the shoreline. *(good)* `Source: Sfiha Coastal Infrastructure Guide`

---

### 10. Corniche Sabadia (id: al-hoceima-corniche-sabadia)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Barriered Walkway — Wide, well-illuminated seaside pedestrian promenade equipped with sturdy protective railings along the coastal drop-off. *(good)* `Source: Protection Civile Al Hoceima`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Purely public civic promenade without commercial touts, aggressive vendors, or ticket hassles. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Municipal Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Stroll — Free 24/7 public access for walking, jogging, and sunset watching; adjoining cafés offer standard local drink pricing. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Database`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Sunset Gathering — A favorite local promenade where Riffian families and joggers take in evening breezes and panoramic Mediterranean views. *(good)* `Source: City Center Cultural Survey`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Paved Rolling — Smooth paved surface throughout with mild coastal rolling undulations, completely accessible for wheelchairs, prams, and seniors. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Urban Mobility Report`

---

### 11. Jardin Al Mnikar (id: al-hoceima-jardin-al-mnikar)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Enclosed Garden — Small, peaceful municipal green space in central Al Hoceima with open visibility and daytime maintenance workers. *(good)* `Source: Commune d'Al Hoceima Service Espaces Verts`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Public municipal park with zero touts, souvenir sellers, or unaccredited service operators. *(good)* `Source: Municipal Civic Registry`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Entry — Open freely to all residents and visitors with no entry fees or admission checkpoints. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Al Hoceima Guide`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Civic Oasis — Authentic neighborhood garden planted with palm trees and flowerbeds, serving as a tranquil gathering spot for locals. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Urban Development Review`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Level Walk — Flat paved paths with abundant shaded benches, requiring virtually no physical effort and suitable for all ages. *(good)* `Source: City Mobility Directory`

---

### 12. Plage Tala Youssef (id: al-hoceima-tala-youssef-beach)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Guarded Bay — Clear, sheltered bay with seasonal lifeguards; rocky edges call for water shoes, and swimmers should watch for summer jet ski paths. *(good)* `Source: Protection Civile Maritime Watch`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Rental Hawkers — Freelance vendors rent uncertified quad bikes and jet skis at inflated rates without proper safety briefings; verify gear before paying. *(caution)* `Source: Souss-Massa & Northern Police Alerts`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Negotiable Extras — Beach entry is free; parking is 10–20 MAD, while umbrella sets and water rentals require negotiating before taking a seat. *(caution)* `Source: Traveler Field Audits`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Coastal Getaway — Popular coastal haven set between rugged hills, frequented by local vacationers and water sports enthusiasts. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan National Tourist Office`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Pebble Mix — Sand mixed with pebbles along the water's edge, accessed easily via a paved road that leads straight down to beach level. *(good)* `Source: BeachSearcher Northern Morocco`

---

### 13. Corniche Morro Viejo (id: al-hoceima-corniche-morro-viejo)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Fenced Clifftop — Paved clifftop walkway with safety handrails; winds can be strong and climbing outside designated balustrades is dangerous. *(caution)* `Source: Protection Civile Al Hoceima`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Open public corniche with zero commercial touting or tourist traps; just standard parking guardians during peak summer. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Civic Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Promenade — Completely free scenic coastal path offering unobstructed viewpoints over Quemado Bay and the open sea. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco Official`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Historic Panorama — Built along the historical headland during the Spanish era and treasured by locals as Al Hoceima's primary evening walking route. *(good)* `Source: OMAU Architectural Archives`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Mild Gradient — Smooth paved promenade with mild slopes, though side connections down to Quemado beach feature steep stairs. *(good)* `Source: Urban Infrastructure Department`

---

### 14. Plage Matadero (id: al-hoceima-plage-matadero)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Protected Shallows — Small, sheltered rocky cove with calm, crystal-clear water ideal for swimming and snorkeling; watch for submerged rocks. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Scuba Club Safety Guide`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Informal Parking — Informal parking guardians occasionally ask for 10 MAD on the access road; kayak rentals on the beach require checking life vest condition. *(caution)* `Source: Al Hoceima Traveler Advisories`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Completely free public beach; simple kayak and paddle rentals operate at modest hourly rates (~40–70 MAD). *(good)* `Source: Hertz Maroc Coastal Guide`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Local Cove — Quiet, unpretentious cove tucked between cliffs, favored by local neighborhood families and spearfishing enthusiasts. *(good)* `Source: Infos Tourisme Maroc`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Stepped Footpath — Reached via a short descent down steps and a gentle dirt ramp; not equipped for wheelchairs or bulky strollers. *(caution)* `Source: Coastal Access Field Survey`

---

### 15. Boujibar National Park Area (id: al-hoceima-boujibar-national-park)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Signposted Trails — Designated 45-hectare urban-forest eco-park equipped with marked walking circuits, safety signs, and municipal groundskeepers. *(good)* `Source: Agence Nationale des Eaux et Forêts (ANEF)`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — State-funded environmental park created under the Manarat Al Moutawassit program; zero commercial touts or unauthorized fees. *(good)* `Source: Direction Régionale des Eaux et Forêts`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Entry — Open freely to the public for jogging, family picnics, and visiting ecological botanical paths. *(good)* `Source: Commune d'Al Hoceima Public Registry`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Pine Forest — Authentic green lung planted with drought-resistant Aleppo pines, mastic, and wild rosemary, offering genuine Mediterranean nature. *(good)* `Source: MAP Express Environmental Report`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Rolling Hikes — Mix of paved trails and graded dirt paths with rolling hills and mild inclines; requires comfortable walking or athletic shoes. *(good)* `Source: Boujibar Trail Infrastructure Review`

---

### 16. Cala Iris Beach & Village (id: al-hoceima-plage-cala-iris)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Sheltered Bay — Naturally protected by offshore islets, creating wave-free, shallow waters; very remote, so carry basic personal first-aid supplies. *(good)* `Source: Protection Civile Beni Boufrah`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Informal Keepers — Informal parking attendants on dirt lots may request 10–20 MAD; ensure fish price per kilo is agreed on at local skiff shacks. *(caution)* `Source: Brainy Backpackers Field Guide`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Budget Friendly — Free beach access; local eco-camping sites and rustic beachfront fish grills are inexpensive (~40–80 MAD). *(good)* `Source: Amis de Cala Iris Registry`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Fishing Hamlet — Working coastal fishing village on the western edge of the national park, famous for traditional wooden skiffs and rare marine fauna. *(good)* `Source: Parc National d'Al Hoceima Conservation Team`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Sand — Flat, sandy beach with easy entry into shallow water; the main challenge is the winding 50 km mountain drive from Al Hoceima. *(good)* `Source: Rif Coastal Road Survey`

### 🎽 Sport & Active (26)
### 17. Moroccan Diving Center (id: al-hoceima-moroccan-diving-center)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Certified Staff — CMAS and PADI-certified divemasters conduct structured pre-dive briefings, safety drills, and equipment checks from Plage d'Isly. *(good)* `Source: Morroco Diving Center Safety Charter`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Licensed Center — Registered marine diving club operating since 2015; avoid freelance beach touts offering uncertified scuba tank rentals. *(good)* `Source: MesLoisirs.ma / Clubs.ma Directory`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Transparent Rates — First-time discovery dives cost ~400–450 MAD and certified boat dives run ~550 MAD, with gear and boat transfer included. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Bucceo Dive Rates`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Local Specialists — Managed by local Riffian dive instructors specializing in the conservation of Bokkoya marine life and Posidonia meadows. *(good)* `Source: Parc National Marin d'Al Hoceima Records`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Beginner Friendly — Intro sessions stay in shallow, calm waters with 1-on-1 supervision; requires basic water comfort and the ability to equalize ear pressure. *(good)* `Source: PADI Discovery Standards`

---

### 18. Scuba Diving in Al Hoceima National Park (id: al-hoceima-diving-experience)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Regulated Depths — Boat dives adhere to Mediterranean marine reserve safety limits, utilizing buddy systems, depth gauges, and on-board emergency oxygen. *(good)* `Source: Direction du Parc National d'Al Hoceima`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Accredited Boats — Coordinated strictly through licensed dive centers at the port or Isly Beach; private uncertified skippers cannot legally guide tank dives. *(good)* `Source: Marine Royale / ANP Port Regulations`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** All Inclusive — Standard offshore reserve dive packages run ~550 MAD per person, transparently covering boat transit, tanks, weights, and guide fees. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Audits`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Marine Sanctuary — Authentic ecological immersion inside a protected Mediterranean Marine Protected Area (MPA) with sea caves and dusky groupers. *(good)* `Source: MedPAN Marine Protected Areas Network`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Certified Divers — Open-water currents and cave entries require prior certification or a completed introductory dive course; not suitable for asthma sufferers. *(caution)* `Source: CMAS Open Water Protocols`

---

### 19. Sea Kayaking & Coastal Caves Tour (id: al-hoceima-kayak-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Lifejacket Equipped — Guides issue mandatory buoyancy vests, sealed dry bags, and emergency whistles before guiding paddlers along limestone bluffs. *(good)* `Source: Association AGIR Ecotourisme`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Uninspected Rentals — Unofficial beach touts rent damaged sit-on-top kayaks without lifejackets; book through recognized eco-guides or port nautical clubs. *(caution)* `Source: Al Hoceima Coastal Tourism Advisories`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Clear Rates — Guided 2–3 hour cave exploration tours run 150–250 MAD per person, while standard hourly rentals at the beach are 50–80 MAD. *(good)* `Source: Local Cooperative Field Rates`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Coastal Wonders — Genuine eco-tour exploring secluded sea caves and karst arches along the Bokkoya massif that are inaccessible by foot. *(good)* `Source: UNESCO / Ramsar Coastal Survey`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Upper Body — Requires 2 to 3 hours of continuous paddling against mild Mediterranean currents; requires moderate shoulder stamina and basic swimming ability. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Nautical Sports Guidelines`

---

### 20. Rif Croisieres Boat Tour (id: al-hoceima-rif-croisieres-boat)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Licensed Vessel — The dedicated passenger shuttle "Azur" operates with certified maritime safety gear, lifebuoys, and licensed skippers from the main port. *(good)* `Source: Ministère du Transport et de la Logistique`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Gate — Managed directly at the Port of Al Hoceima ticket counter since 2010; prevents unauthorized boarding on unregulated fishing skiffs. *(good)* `Source: Petit Futé Al Hoceima / BizNiz.ma`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Published Tariffs — Fixed excursion fares (~100–150 MAD per seat on group coastal trips, ~320 MAD for extended private reserve tours). *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Rif Croisières Desk`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Longest Running — The pioneer maritime tour operator in Al Hoceima, offering genuine historical commentary on the Spanish islets and cliffs. *(good)* `Source: Regional Tourism Delegation`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Seated Leisure — Completely passive scenic boating requiring zero physical exertion, fully accessible for seniors, small children, and non-swimmers. *(good)* `Source: Maritime Passenger Accessibility Standards`

---

### 21. Guided Hiking in Al Hoceima National Park (id: al-hoceima-hiking-national-park)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Trail Navigation — Certified eco-guides carry first-aid gear and navigate steep karst ravines safely away from crumbling cliff edges. *(good)* `Source: Agence Nationale des Eaux et Forêts`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unlicensed Guides — Freelancers at road intersections solicit tourists without park authorization; hire certified mountain guides through official eco-associations. *(caution)* `Source: Association Nationale des Guides de Tourisme`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Established Fees — Standard full-day guided treks cost 200–350 MAD per group/person, with clear inclusions for local village tea and park coordination. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa & Northern Trekking Rates`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Village Immersion — Authentic cultural trekking connecting remote Amazigh douars (hamlets), terraced olive orchards, and wild osprey habitats. *(good)* `Source: Parc National d'Al Hoceima Conservation Team`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Demanding Trek — 4 to 6 hours over rough limestone scree, dry ravines, and steep ascents; requires solid hiking boots and sun protection. *(caution)* `Source: High Rif Mountain Trail Survey`

---

### 22. Surf Lesson at Sfiha Beach (id: al-hoceima-surf-lesson)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Foam Boards — Beginner lessons utilize soft-top foam boards with safety ankle leashes and neoprene wetsuits in waist-deep water. *(good)* `Source: Fédération Royale Marocaine de Surf`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unvetted Touts — Avoid informal beach boys renting warped surfboards without leashes; book through structured seasonal surf camps or instructors. *(caution)* `Source: Sfiha Beach Operations Advisory`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Transparent Pricing — Lessons are clearly priced at 200–280 MAD for a 1.5–2 hour session, with board and wetsuit rental included in the fee. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Surf School Audits`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Developing Scene — Emerging Mediterranean surf community riding winter and spring sandbar swells with views of the Peñón de Alhucemas. *(good)* `Source: Top Morocco Travel Surf Guide`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Beginner Accessible — Sand-bottom breaks make standing up gentle for first-timers, requiring moderate core balance and paddle endurance. *(good)* `Source: ISA Beginner Coaching Standards`

---

### 23. Traditional Fishing Experience (id: al-hoceima-fishing-charter)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Marine Registered — Licensed wooden trawlers or artisanal skiffs equipped with passenger life vests, marine VHF radio, and seasoned local captains. *(good)* `Source: Office National des Pêches (ONP)`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Cooperative Booked — Coordinate directly with recognized artisanal fishing cooperatives at the port to avoid shortened trips or unagreed fuel charges. *(caution)* `Source: Délégation des Pêches Maritimes Al Hoceima`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Set Charter — Shared half-day artisanal fishing outings run ~350–500 MAD per angler, including handlines, bait, tackle, and keeping part of the catch. *(good)* `Source: Port Tourism Services Registry`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Ancestral Line — Genuine cultural participation in centuries-old Riffian artisanal fishing methods using traditional handlines and gillnets. *(good)* `Source: Mediterranean Artisanal Fisheries Network`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Motion Sensitive — Physically undemanding while fishing, but open Mediterranean swells can trigger acute motion sickness for sensitive passengers. *(caution)* `Source: Maritime Health Advisories`

---

### 24. Snorkeling at Cala Iris (id: al-hoceima-snorkeling-cala-iris)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Sheltered Shallows — The bay is sheltered from open waves by offshore islets, with tours providing fitted snorkel vests, masks, and fins. *(good)* `Source: Amis de Cala Iris Marine Safety`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Leaky Masks — Avoid informal beach kiosks renting ill-fitting, scratched snorkel gear; book with professional guides who provide sanitized equipment. *(caution)* `Source: Regional Eco-Tourism Field Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Modest Package — Guided 3-hour snorkeling outings run ~260 MAD per person including gear and boat access; self-guided snorkeling off the beach is free. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Untouched Cove — Pristine marine biodiversity haven harboring vibrant Posidonia oceanica beds, red starfish, and rare Mediterranean limpets. *(good)* `Source: Parc National d'Al Hoceima Biodiversity Records`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Swims — Easy swimming in clear, shallow waters, highly suited for families and beginners with basic water comfort. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Coastal Snorkeling Index`

---

### 25. Paragliding over the Rif Coast (id: al-hoceima-paragliding)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Certified Pilots — Tandem flights flown with certified instructors using inspected dual-seat wings, reserve parachutes, and strict wind monitoring. *(good)* `Source: Fédération Royale Marocaine de l'Aviation Légère`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Verified Launches — Coordinate with established tandem pilots taking off from approved Morro Viejo or Sabadia cliffs; confirm flight duration beforehand. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / MesLoisirs.ma`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Flight — Tandem coastal flights are fixed at roughly 850 MAD per passenger (~15–20 minutes flight time), often including flight video footage. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Tariff Board`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Aerial Panorama — Spectacular aerial flight delivering panoramic views of the Mediterranean coastline, limestone cliffs, and city bays. *(good)* `Source: Northern Morocco Adventure Tourism`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Running Stride — Passengers need zero piloting skill, requiring only a brief 5-meter running stride upon launch and a standing landing on the beach. *(good)* `Source: Tandem Paragliding Standards`

---

### 26. 4x4 Jeep Safari in National Park (id: al-hoceima-jeep-tour-park)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Rugged Vehicles — Conducted in four-wheel-drive vehicles driven by professional local drivers trained for mountainous dirt tracks. *(good)* `Source: Agence Nationale des Eaux et Forêts`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unlicensed Street — Avoid unauthorized private drivers offering "safaris" in passenger sedans that cannot reach remote park vistas. *(caution)* `Source: Al Hoceima Transport Regulations`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Rate — Half-day 4-hour guided safari packages are fixed at 380 MAD per person, including driver, fuel, and park viewpoints. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Tours`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Cultural Safari — Traverses remote mountain tracks across the Bokkoya massif, passing isolated adobe farmsteads and coastal viewpoints. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa & Rif Ecotourism Guide`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Bumpy Ride — Physically relaxed seated tour, but traverses unpaved mountain switchbacks and bumpy dirt trails that can cause mild motion discomfort. *(caution)* `Source: Rural Road Transit Survey`

### 27. Coastal Bike Tour (id: al-hoceima-bike-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Helmet Provided — Guided tours include fitted cycling helmets, geared mountain bikes or e-bikes, and safety marshals along paved coastal roads. *(good)* `Source: Fédération Royale Marocaine de Cyclisme`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Checked Brakes — Inspect bike brakes, derailleur cables, and tire wear before leaving; book with registered rental shops to prevent unfair repair claims. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Cycling Club Advisories`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Standard Rates — Guided 2–3 hour coastal rides cost ~180–250 MAD, while independent bike rentals average 50–80 MAD per half-day. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Rental Kiosks`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Eco Exploration — Low-impact ride connecting Corniche Sabadia, Cala Bonita, and coastal cliffs, popular with local cycling clubs. *(good)* `Source: Northern Rif Green Tourism Initiative`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Rolling Incline — Coastal topography features sustained hilly undulations; requires moderate leg and cardiovascular stamina unless using an e-bike. *(caution)* `Source: Al Hoceima Topographical Cycling Survey`

* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Front Desk — Direct walk-in counter registration on Rue Abou Al Kacem Zahraoui; no subscription contracts or third-party sales touts. *(good)* `Source: Telecontact.ma / Moroccan Mate`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Flat Rate — Walk-in gym day pass is clearly fixed at 40 MAD per person, granting full access to weight floors and shower facilities. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Boxing Legacy — Renowned local athletic institution founded in 1988, famed for training Moroccan champion kickboxers like Naoufal Benazzouz. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Full-Contact Kickboxing Federation`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** High Strenuous — Gym floor machines allow self-directed training, whereas heavy bag and kickboxing circuits are physically demanding. *(caution)* `Source: Combat Training Standards`
