* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Aquatic Therapy — Authentic local aquatic center widely used by neighborhood residents, women, and seniors for joint-friendly exercise. *(good)* `Source: Al Hoceima Urban Health Directory`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Zero Impact — Water buoyancy supports body weight and eliminates joint stress, making aquagym sessions effortless on knees and hips for all ages. *(good)* `Source: Hydrotherapy Exercise Guidelines`

---

### 42. Power House Gym (id: al-hoceima-power-house-gym)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Spotter Assisted — Heavy-duty power cages, calibrated iron plates, and experienced lifters on the gym floor available for safety spotting. *(good)* `Source: Power House Gym Equipment Audit`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Transparent Walk-In — No-frills neighborhood gym near Sfiha; simple cash walk-in passes with zero administrative surcharges or contracts. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Directory`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Budget Fixed — Single-session entry is fixed at 40 MAD flat rate per person, covering access to free weights, plate machines, and benches. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Iron Powerlifting — Authentic, old-school strength gym dedicated purely to heavy bodybuilding, powerlifting, and local iron culture. *(good)* `Source: Regional Strength Athletes Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Heavy Exertion — Focuses on heavy compound resistance training, requiring significant muscular effort, though lifters can select their own weights. *(caution)* `Source: Powerlifting Training Standards`


## Asilah — 44 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/asilah/` — 8 heritage · 6 nature · 29 sport · 1 wellness

### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (8)
### 1. Asilah Medina & Art Murals (id: asilah-medina)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Pedestrian Zone — Fully pedestrianized, exceptionally clean whitewashed streets with active municipal lighting and family-friendly security. *(good)* `Source: Commune d'Asilah Urban Safety`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Low Hassle — One of Morocco's most relaxed medinas; minimal touting, no aggressive shopkeepers, and zero faux-guide harassment. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco / Story Rabat`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Completely free self-guided exploration of the open-air streets and rotating wall murals year-round. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Cultural Foundation`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Living Gallery — Genuine contemporary art hub celebrated through the annual Asilah Cultural Moussem (since 1978), where artists repaint walls each summer. *(good)* `Source: Fondation du Forum d'Asilah`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Flat Stroll — Level paved streets and gentle stone flags with virtually no stairs; exceptionally easy for wheelchairs, strollers, and seniors. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Accessibility Survey`

---

### 2. Portuguese Ramparts & Borj Krikia (id: asilah-ramparts)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Low Parapets — Elevated oceanfront bastion platform has low protective stone ledges; strong Atlantic wind gusts and wet stones during high tide require care. *(caution)* `Source: Protection Civile Asilah`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Low Risk — Occasional self-appointed locals offering sunset photos or impromptu history for small tips, but entirely avoidable and low-pressure. *(good)* `Source: Traveler Field Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — 100% free public access along the ramparts, sea battlements, and the Borj Krikia sunset viewpoint. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Historic Bastion — Original 15th-century Portuguese ocean artillery bastion and defensive stone walls facing the crashing Atlantic waves. *(good)* `Source: Ministère de la Culture`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Incline — Walkable rampart paths with a few shallow, broad stone steps leading up to the artillery platform; manageable for almost all fitness levels. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Walking Guide`

---

### 3. Palais Raissouni (Centre Hassan II des Rencontres Internationales) (id: asilah-palais-raissouni)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Regulated Interior — Formal municipal cultural center with security guards at the door, well-maintained Andalusian courtyards, and clean marble floors. *(good)* `Source: Centre Hassan II Administration`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Venue — Managed by the Fondation du Forum d'Asilah; no touts, fake guides, or unaccredited fees. *(good)* `Source: Fondation du Forum d'Asilah Records`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Entry — Public entry to art exhibitions and courtyard salons during cultural colloquia and the Moussem festival is completely free. *(good)* `Source: Culturama / Moroccan Mate`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Historic Palace — Built in the early 20th century by chieftain Moulay Ahmed Raissouni; retains authentic carved stucco, cedar ceilings, and marble fountains. *(good)* `Source: Archnet Cultural Heritage`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Mostly Level — Ground floor and central courtyard are flat with gentle thresholds, though accessing upper balconies involves historic stone staircases. *(good)* `Source: Architectural Survey Asilah`

---

### 4. Borj Al-Kamra (Portuguese Keep) (id: asilah-borj-al-kamra)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Secure Perimeter — Located in the open, pedestrian-friendly Place Ibn Khaldoun beside Bab al-Bahr; well-lit with clear open sightlines. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Heritage Police`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Prominent historic civic landmark with no touts or commercial ticketing traps; interior is closed except during special cultural exhibitions. *(good)* `Source: Asilah City Guide`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Viewing — Viewing the Portuguese keep and its four corbelled watchtowers from the outside is free at all hours. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Manueline Keep — 1509 Portuguese Manueline defense tower (Torre de Menagem) restored with original cut-stone corners and medieval bartizans. *(good)* `Source: Ministère de la Culture`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Zero Incline — Completely flat paved square (Place Ibn Khaldoun) with zero physical hurdles, fully accessible to everyone. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Urban Infrastructure`

---

### 5. Cimetière Marin Musulman & Mausolée Sidi Ahmed El Mansour (id: asilah-mausolee-sidi-ahmed-mansour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Rampart Overlook — Perched between the Portuguese ramparts and crashing ocean rocks; viewing from the fortified promenade is safe, but descending onto seaside boulders is risky. *(caution)* `Source: Protection Civile Asilah`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Solemn religious site free of touts; non-Muslim visitors should view respectfully from the ramparts above rather than intruding into the cemetery. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Religious Tourism Guidelines`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Viewing — Free public overlook from the Borj Krikia ramparts; no entry fees or permits required. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Henri Trip`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Sacred Ground — Centuries-old Atlantic Muslim cemetery featuring vibrant multi-colored zellij ceramic gravestones and the whitewashed dome of Sidi Ahmed ben Mansour. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Historical Society`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Flat Overlook — Viewing from the stone ramparts requires only an easy flat walk; walking inside the cemetery is restricted and involves uneven natural ground. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Mobility Audit`

---

### 6. Jewish Cemetery of Asilah (id: asilah-jewish-cemetery)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Walled Grounds — Enclosed by protective masonry walls on Chemin Rmilat near the sea cliffs, cared for by a resident guardian. *(good)* `Source: Jewish Community of Tangier Caretaker Records`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Guardian Tipped — Gated site with a resident caretaker; call the posted gate number to enter, and a small discretionary gratuity (20–50 MAD) is customary for grounds maintenance. *(good)* `Source: Atlas Obscura / Bazar Travels`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Discretionary Tip — No official entrance fee; visitors typically leave a modest voluntary tip to the caretaker. *(good)* `Source: Bazar Travels Field Guide`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Historic Necropolis — Centuries-old Sephardic resting place featuring whitewashed tombs with Hebrew and Spanish epitaphs, including the historic grave of Simi Roiff (1944). *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Culture Ministry National Heritage List`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Uneven Earth — Reached via a gentle 5-minute walk from the medina; interior grounds have dirt paths and low, uneven gravestones requiring careful footing. *(caution)* `Source: Tangier-Asilah Heritage Survey`

---

### 7. Dar Al Khattat (Calligraphy Studio) (id: asilah-dar-al-khattat)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Safe Atelier — Peaceful, clean indoor residential art atelier and workshop located in the safe medina alley of Rue Borj Al Ghoula. *(good)* `Source: Visit Asilah Studio Standards`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Artist Direct — Independent workshop run directly by master calligrapher Sadik Haddari; zero aggressive souvenir hawkers or middleman markups. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco Map`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Transparent Art — Browsing the studio and observing demonstrations is free; custom calligraphy pieces and workshops carry clear, agreed-upon prices. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Terre d'Artistes`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Master Craft — Dedicated to classical Arabic, Maghrebi, and Andalusian calligraphy scripts using traditional hand-cut reeds and soot-based inks. *(good)* `Source: Fondation du Forum d'Asilah Arts Archive`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step Entry — Located within a ground-floor medina residence; involves flat medina walking with only a low doorway step at the entrance. *(good)* `Source: Medina Walk Accessibility Records`

---

### 8. Site Archéologique de Zilil / Dchar Jdid (id: asilah-site-archeologique-zilil)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Unguarded Ruins — Open rural archaeological ruins with no safety fences, open cisterns, overgrown vegetation, and no active on-site medical post. *(caution)* `Source: INSAP Archaeological Survey`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Informal Guides — Local villagers from Dchar Jdid may offer unsolicited guiding; verify their knowledge before tipping, or explore independently. *(caution)* `Source: Le360 Heritage Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Ruins — Open countryside archaeological site with no formal ticket booth or entry fee. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / INSAP`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Roman Colony — Genuine ruins of *Iulia Constantia Zilil*, an Augustan Roman-Berber colony founded between 33 and 25 BC, featuring a 4th-century paleo-Christian basilica, thermae, and cisterns. *(good)* `Source: École Française de Rome / INSAP`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Rough Countryside — Unpaved dirt trails, tall grass, and uneven stone foundations situated 13 km northeast of Asilah; requires sturdy walking boots, vehicle transport, and sun protection. *(caution)* `Source: Northern Morocco Archaeological Trail Survey`

### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (6)
### 9. Paradise Beach / Plage Rmilat (id: asilah-paradise-beach)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Strong Undertow — Open Atlantic shoreline with powerful breakers and dangerous rip currents; lifeguards are present only during July and August. *(caution)* `Source: Protection Civile Asilah`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Calèche Touts — Horse carriage (calèche) and taxi drivers frequently demand inflated return fares; lock in the round-trip price and departure time before leaving. *(caution)* `Source: Traveler Field Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Shore — Beach access is completely free; round-trip horse carriage or taxi hire costs ~150–200 MAD, while seasonal parasols rent for 30–50 MAD. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco Map`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Bohemian Haven — Striking golden-sand beach framed by tall red sandstone cliffs, historically cherished as an uncrowded bohemian summer retreat. *(good)* `Source: Regional Tourism Delegation`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Steep Descent — Requires a bumpy carriage or vehicle ride over dirt roads followed by a steep 10-minute downhill footpath to the sand. *(caution)* `Source: Asilah Coastal Hiking Survey`

---

### 10. Asilah Town Beach (id: asilah-main-beach)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Lifeguarded Shallows — Partially sheltered by the harbor breakwater with seasonal lifeguards on duty; stay clear of boat channels near the jetty. *(good)* `Source: Protection Civile Tanger-Tétouan`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Camel Hustlers — Beachfront camel and pony handlers solicit tourists with flexible quotes; negotiate the ride price before mounting. *(caution)* `Source: Asilah Tourist Police Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Budget Friendly — Completely free public beach; parasol and chair rentals run 30–50 MAD for the day, and short camel rides cost 30–50 MAD. *(good)* `Source: Commune d'Asilah Municipal Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Civic Shoreline — Energetic municipal beachfront where local Moroccan families and youths gather daily for football matches against the medina walls. *(good)* `Source: Barceló Travel Guide Asilah`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step-Free Sand — Flat, broad sandy beach accessed directly from the paved coastal promenade, fully manageable for strollers and all walking levels. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Urban Infrastructure Review`

---

### 11. Sidi Mghayet Beach (id: asilah-sidi-mghayet-beach)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Wild Breakers — Remote open ocean beach subject to heavy waves and strong undertows; completely unpatrolled with no medical posts nearby. *(caution)* `Source: Asilah Maritime Safety Advisory`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Negotiated Rides — Grand taxi drivers quote high rates for the remote route; agree firmly on a round-trip fare (~250–350 MAD) including waiting time. *(caution)* `Source: Petit Futé Asilah`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Free public access; rustic bamboo-thatched beach shacks serve fresh grilled fish and mint tea at straightforward local prices (~60–100 MAD). *(good)* `Source: Zaila.com / Guide du Maroc`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Untamed Inlet — Untouched natural beach flanked by rolling green hills and olive groves, largely spared from commercial mass tourism. *(good)* `Source: Asilah.city Guide`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Rough Track — Situated 12–15 km south of Asilah, requiring travel over a bumpy unpaved dirt road (high-clearance car recommended) and loose sand walking. *(caution)* `Source: Northern Atlantic Coastal Trail Survey`

---

### 12. Briech Beach (id: asilah-briech-beach)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Estuary Currents — Wide shoreline where Oued Briech forms a calm river mouth lagoon, though Atlantic surf breaks have brisk seasonal currents. *(caution)* `Source: Surf-Forecast Briech Safety Index`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Minimal Touting — Laid-back rural beach free of aggressive touting; occasional informal parking guardians ask 10–15 MAD on dirt turnouts. *(good)* `Source: Briech Beach Guide`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Shore — 100% free open public beach; small roadside seafood shacks along the nearby bridge serve grilled sardines for 40–70 MAD. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco Map Briech`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Local Haven — Peaceful estuary and dune setting frequented by local Moroccan surfers, fishermen, and families looking for quiet coastlines. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa & Northern Morocco Surf Review`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Flat — Broad, level sandy plain reached via a short flat track off the N1 highway; requires minimal physical effort. *(good)* `Source: Briech Coastal Infrastructure Guide`

---

### 13. Mezora Cromlech (id: asilah-mezora-cromlech)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Exposed Stones — Rural archaeological mound of 167 standing megaliths in open fields; totally exposed to sun and wind with no shade or water. *(caution)* `Source: Ministère de la Culture Maroc / INSAP`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Informal Touts — Village youths may approach offering unofficial guided tours; look for the recognized site caretaker and decline third-party fees. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Modest Gratuity — Entry is nominally free, but offering a modest gratuity (~10–20 MAD) to the on-site guardian who unlocks the gate is customary. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Prehistoric Monolith — The largest megalithic stone circle and burial tumulus in northwest Africa, dating back to the Mauretanian era (4th–3rd century BC). *(good)* `Source: INSAP National Heritage Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Uneven Earth — Located 15 km southeast of Asilah via rural roads and 3 km of rough dirt tracks; requires walking over uneven stony grass. *(caution)* `Source: Northern Morocco Archaeological Survey`

---

### 14. Asilah Seawall Promenade (id: asilah-promenade)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Secure Promenade — Wide, well-illuminated pedestrian avenue protected by sea parapets; winter high-tide storms can cast sea spray onto walkway tiles. *(good)* `Source: Commune d'Asilah Infrastructure`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Scam-Free — Public municipal promenade free from aggressive sales or tourist traps; carriage rides across town require basic advance negotiation. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Urban Security Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Walkway — Open public access 24/7 at no cost; terrace cafés and patisseries lining the avenue offer transparent menu pricing. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Database`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Sunset Paseo — The social heart of town where local residents and artists take their evening stroll while watching the sunset over the Atlantic. *(good)* `Source: Barceló Travel Guide`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step-Free Level — Completely flat, smooth paved seaside walkway with zero stairs and seamless curb cuts, fully accessible for wheelchairs and seniors. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Urban Accessibility Report`

### 🎽 Sport & Active (29)
### 15. Surf Lesson with Antik Surf Club (id: asilah-surf-lesson)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Certified Instructors — Experienced local coaches provide buoyant foam beginner boards, fitted wetsuits, and Atlantic rip-current safety briefings. *(good)* `Source: Antik Surf Club Safety Standards`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Established Shop — Recognized independent surf club based on Rue Ben Marzouk; fixed bookings with zero unvetted beach touts. *(good)* `Source: BizNiz.ma / Antik Surf Registry`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Tariff — Lessons are fixed at 250 MAD (~€25) per person, covering surfboard, wetsuit, and personalized coaching. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Antik Surf Desk`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Grassroots Surf — Genuine local surf initiative founded by Moroccan shapers and surfers dedicated to Atlantic wave culture and hand-shaped boards. *(good)* `Source: Atlantik Documentary / Sessions Travel`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Beginner Friendly — Practiced in waist-deep whitewater over sandy beachbreaks; requires basic swimming ability and modest paddling stamina. *(good)* `Source: ISA Surf Coaching Standards`

---

### 16. Horseback Riding on Paradise Beach (id: asilah-horseback-riding)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Equipped Stables — Guided rides include fitted riding helmets, well-cared-for horses, and local handlers supervising riders along the tideline. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Equestrian Tourism Guidelines`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Booking — Informal riders on the beach may quote arbitrary prices; book through verified operators or riad desks to guarantee ride duration. *(caution)* `Source: Desert Merzouga Tours / Asilah Field Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Clear Packages — Standard guided 1.5-hour beach rides cost 280 MAD (~€28) per rider with gear and local guide included. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Local Horsemanship — Run by local equestrian families passionate about Arabian-Berber horses and sharing the open Atlantic shoreline. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Coastal Tourism Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Stride — Calm walking pace along the packed sand makes it suitable for complete beginners and children, with galloping reserved for experienced riders. *(good)* `Source: Equine Tourism Mobility Standards`

---

### 17. Coastal Boat Tour & Fishing (id: asilah-boat-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Equipped Vessel — Licensed coastal motorboat equipped with passenger life jackets, marine VHF radio, and seasoned local skippers departing from Asilah Port. *(good)* `Source: Direction des Pêches Maritimes Asilah`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Port Regulated — Official departure from Puerto Marina Asilah; prevents uninspected private skiff touting along the jetty. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Puerto Marina Registry`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Excursion — 3-hour coastal outing costs 320 MAD per person, including fishing lines, bait, swimming stops, and life vests. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Tariff Board`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Artisanal Fishery — Genuine maritime trip showcasing Portuguese sea ramparts from the water, coastal caves, and authentic handline fishing techniques. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Maritime Heritage Bureau`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Seated Low-Impact — Relaxed seated coastal boating requiring no physical exertion, though open Atlantic swells can induce motion sickness for some. *(caution)* `Source: Coastal Boating Advisory`

---

### 18. Quad Bike Adventure (id: asilah-quad-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Safety Briefed — Includes mandatory protective full-face helmets, eyewear, automatic easy-to-drive quad bikes, and lead marshals. *(good)* `Source: Quad Tanger Assilah Safety Protocols`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Licensed Operator — Book with registered excursion providers to avoid uninsured street rental bikes and unauthorized trail deviations. *(caution)* `Source: Regional Tourism Delegation`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Tour — 2-hour guided off-road quad tour is fixed at 380 MAD (~€35–40) per driver, with transparent second-passenger rates. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Quad Biking Asilah Tariff`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Countryside Trails — Scenic off-road route tracing rolling pastoral farmlands, Atlantic sand dunes, and secluded coastal viewpoints south of town. *(good)* `Source: Northern Morocco Adventure Tourism`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Moderate Vibration — Simple thumb-throttle automatic operation, though handling sandy ruts and dirt tracks causes sustained body vibration and dust. *(caution)* `Source: Off-Road ATV Safety Index`

---

### 19. Coastal Bike Tour (id: asilah-bike-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Helmet Provided — Quality geared hybrid/mountain bikes provided with fitted safety helmets, water bottle holders, and local guides leading the route. *(good)* `Source: Fédération Royale Marocaine de Cyclisme`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Booking — Organized departures directly from the medina gates; pre-ride inspection eliminates surprise equipment damage disputes. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Tariff — 2-hour guided coastal cycling excursion is fixed at 180 MAD per person, covering bike rental, helmet, and guide. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Official Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Local Sightseeing — Scenic pedal along the seaside corniche, mural-painted medina perimeter, and rustic village roads toward surrounding beaches. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Cultural Tourism Guide`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Rolling Slopes — Mostly flat paved surfaces with gentle coastal undulations; requires basic cycling balance and mild cardiovascular stamina. *(good)* `Source: Coastal Cycling Mobility Survey`

---

### 20. Camel Ride on Paradise Beach (id: asilah-camel-ride)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Lead Reins — Calmer dromedaries led on foot by experienced Berber handlers along the wide, flat hard-packed Atlantic tideline. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Equestrian & Camel Tourism`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Hustler Caution — Independent handlers roaming the sand often negotiate loose quotes; agree firmly on the full price and duration before mounting. *(caution)* `Source: Traveler Field Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Clear Tariff — Standard 1-hour sunset camel ride is fixed at 150 MAD per person when booked directly or through reputable contacts. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Scenic Shoreline — Classic Moroccan seaside pastime set against the dramatic red cliffs and wide golden sands of Rmilat. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa & Northern Morocco Guides`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Balance — Requires basic hip flexibility to stay balanced when the camel kneels and stands; otherwise completely relaxing and beginner-friendly. *(good)* `Source: Animal Riding Standards`

---

### 21. Traditional Fishing Charter (id: asilah-fishing-charter)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Marine Inspected — Registered traditional wooden fishing craft equipped with mandatory lifejackets, marine safety beacons, and local captains. *(good)* `Source: Office National des Pêches (ONP)`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Harbor — Coordinated directly via the artisanal fishing harbor cooperative, eliminating informal touts and shortened itineraries. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Port Authority`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Charter — Half-day 4-hour artisanal charter is fixed at 300 MAD per angler, including handlines, bait, tackle, and keeping catch. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Database`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Centuries-Old Craft — Authentic participation alongside traditional Riffian fishermen utilizing age-old handline and bottom-fishing techniques. *(good)* `Source: Mediterranean Artisanal Fishing Guild`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Motion Sensitive — Physically gentle angling, but Atlantic ocean chop and groundswells can trigger acute motion sickness. *(caution)* `Source: Maritime Health Advisories`

---

### 22. Coastal Kayak Adventure (id: asilah-kayak-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Buoyant Vests — Stable sit-on-top sea kayaks equipped with certified buoyancy vests, watertight gear bags, and guided navigation along rock formations. *(good)* `Source: Association Nautique Asilah`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unvetted Kiosks — Avoid informal beach operators renting cracked kayaks without life jackets; book structured guided excursions. *(caution)* `Source: Asilah Coastal Safety Advisory`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Package — 2.5-hour guided sea kayak cave tour is fixed at 260 MAD per person, including gear, guide, and safety orientation. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Official Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Secluded Coves — Eco-friendly paddling into secluded sea caverns, rocky arches, and hidden limestone inlets south of the medina walls. *(good)* `Source: Northern Morocco Eco-Guide Guild`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Upper Body — Involves continuous rhythmic paddling against coastal Atlantic tides; requires moderate arm and core stamina and swimming confidence. *(caution)* `Source: Nautical Sports Physical Matrix`

---

### 23. Guided Coastal Hike (id: asilah-coastal-hike)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Trail Guided — Local guides navigate marked cliff trails, maintain safe distances from cliff edges, and provide first-aid kits and trail water. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Association of Mountain Guides`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Accredited Guide — Book with licensed local guides departing from the medina to avoid unvetted escorts and vague route promises. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Asilah Heritage Center`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Rate — 3-hour private guided hike is fixed at 180 MAD per person, including trail guide, bottled water, and local snacks. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Coastal Heritage — Authentic walking trail passing coastal shrines, shepherd trails, wildflower meadows, and untamed beaches. *(good)* `Source: Souss-Massa & Northern Eco-Hiking Survey`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Moderate Trail — Dirt trails, loose shale, and undulating cliff paths; requires comfortable walking sneakers or hiking shoes and sun protection. *(good)* `Source: Rif Coastal Trail Matrix`

* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Marina Counter — Departures arranged directly through Puerto Marina Asilah with documented boarding times; zero dockside ticket scalping. *(good)* `Source: Puerto Marina Asilah Administration`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Clear Tariff — 2-hour sunset sail is fixed at 350 MAD per passenger, including life vests, refreshing drinks, and light Moroccan snacks. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Golden Hour — Peaceful nautical experience offering iconic views of the sun setting into the Atlantic behind the 15th-century Portuguese battlements. *(good)* `Source: Northern Morocco Tourism Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Effortless Seated — Relaxed seated scenic sailing with zero physical exertion, fully suitable for families, couples, and seniors. *(good)* `Source: Marine Tourism Accessibility Survey`

* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Scenic Eco-Ride — Quiet, eco-friendly transit exploring the medina perimeter, seaside promenades, and rural cliff routes toward Paradise Beach. *(good)* `Source: Asilah Green Tourism Initiative`

## Casablanca — 43 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/casablanca/` — 11 heritage · 3 nature · 28 sport · 1 wellness
### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (11)
### 1. Hassan II Mosque (id: casablanca-hassan-ii-mosque)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Polished Slippery — Sea spray makes outdoor marble esplanades very slick; interior access involves airport-style metal detectors and bag inspection. *(caution)* `Source: Fondation de la Mosquée Hassan II`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Guided — Tickets must be purchased strictly at the subterranean visitor center; decline touts outside falsely claiming the mosque is closed. *(caution)* `Source: Visit Casablanca Official Portal`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Tariffs — Standard adult entry is fixed at 130 MAD (with student/child discounts), which includes an accredited multilingual interior tour. *(good)* `Source: Fondation de la Mosquée Hassan II Box Office`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Master Artisan — Landmark of authentic Moroccan craftsmanship showcasing hand-carved cedarwood, titanium retractable roof, and zellige tiles by 10,000 artisans. *(good)* `Source: Ministère des Habous et des Affaires Islamiques`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Extensive Walking — Vast distances across the esplanade and prayer hall, requiring 45–60 minutes of standing/walking barefoot or in socks with provided shoe bags. *(caution)* `Source: Mosque Visitor Regulations`

---

### 2. Casablanca Old Medina (id: casablanca-old-medina)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Motorbike Crowded — Narrow working-class alleys shared with speeding motorbikes and handcarts; stay alert for pickpockets, particularly around Bab Marrakech after dark. *(caution)* `Source: Sûreté Nationale Casablanca`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Faux Guides — Unofficial "guides" often loiter near gates offering tours to non-existent tanneries or leading tourists into commission-based leather shops. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Tourist Police Advisories`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Heavy Bargaining — Free public access; goods and souvenirs lack price tags, demanding firm price negotiation down to a fraction of the initial quote. *(caution)* `Source: Lonely Planet Casablanca`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Living Quarter — Unvarnished 19th-century residential quarter with local communal bread ovens, tailor workshops, and neighborhood produce stalls rather than a tourist facade. *(good)* `Source: Casamémoire Heritage Inventory`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Uneven Paving — Mostly flat terrain, but cracked flagstones, raised curbs, and crowded pathways make navigation awkward for strollers and wheelchairs. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Urban Mobility Review`

---

### 3. Place Mohammed V (id: casablanca-mohammed-v-square)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Heavily Patrolled — Prominent civic square facing government halls (Wilaya and Palais de Justice) with constant municipal police presence and bright illumination. *(good)* `Source: Wilaya de la Région de Casablanca-Settat`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Photo Solicitation — Traditional water sellers (guerrab) in bright red attire and pigeon-feed vendors demand steep tips if photographed; settle prices first. *(caution)* `Source: Traveler Field Audits`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Completely free public plaza and musical fountain; surrounding boulevard cafés display standard fixed menu prices. *(good)* `Source: Visit Casablanca Public Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Mauresque Heritage — The historic centerpiece of 1920s French protectorate civic planning, blending classical French Beaux-Arts facades with traditional Moroccan Moorish ornamentation. *(good)* `Source: UNESCO Tentative List Casablanca Heritage`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step-Free Level — Broad, level paved promenade with seamless streetcar (tramway) platforms and curb ramps, fully accessible for wheelchairs and strollers. *(good)* `Source: Casa Transports Accessibility Guide`

---

### 4. Quartier Habous / New Medina (id: casablanca-habous-quartier)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Calm Patrolled — Exceptionally clean, tranquil, and orderly neighborhood adjacent to the Royal Palace, monitored regularly by police. *(good)* `Source: Préfecture d'Al Fida-Mers Sultan`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Low Hassle — One of Morocco's least aggressive medina settings; traditional bookshops, olive souks, and pastry vendors operate without touting or pushy sales tactics. *(good)* `Source: Visit Morocco Official`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fair Fixed — Public walking is free; iconic culinary stops like Pâtisserie Bennis Habous and local olive vendors use clear, fixed per-kilo pricing. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Neo-Traditional — Purpose-built in the 1920s by architects Albert Laprade and Auguste Cadet to reinterpret traditional Moroccan stone architecture, souks, and residential riads. *(good)* `Source: Casamémoire Architectural Archives`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Arcades — Flat stone walkways sheltered beneath arched colonnades with minimal stairs, providing effortless walking and sun protection for all ages. *(good)* `Source: Habous District Pedestrian Survey`

---

### 5. Mahkama du Pacha / Palais du Méchouar (id: casablanca-mahkama-du-pacha)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Guarded Courthouse — Functioning regional administrative courthouse and reception venue guarded by municipal security personnel with secure, quiet inner courts. *(good)* `Source: Ministère de la Justice Maroc`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Restricted Access — Independent tourist entry is strictly controlled during judicial hours; avoid outside street touts claiming they can unlock doors without official authorization. *(caution)* `Source: Visit Casablanca Administrative Notes`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Nominal Discretionary — Free municipal entry when accompanied by an accredited city guide, with a customary modest gratuity (~20–30 MAD) given to the on-site guardian. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Casamémoire Field Data`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Hispano-Moorish Masterwork — Completed in 1952 by Auguste Cadet using master craftsmen; features 60+ ornate chambers, Andalusian marble courtyards, and hand-carved cedar ceilings. *(good)* `Source: Archnet Islamic Architecture Archive`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Shallow Thresholds — Ground floor courtyards are level with smooth tile, though navigating between halls involves stepping over shallow carved doorway thresholds. *(good)* `Source: Habous Heritage Accessibility Review`

---

### 6. Marché Central (id: casablanca-marche-central)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Busy Crowded — Bustling covered marketplace located on Boulevard Mohammed V; safe during daytime, though watchful eyes are needed against bag pickpockets in narrow corridors. *(caution)* `Source: Sûreté Urbaine Casablanca`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Grill Markups — Fish stalls in the central courtyard offer to cook your purchases; confirm fish cleaning fees, grilling charges per kilo, and side-dish prices beforehand. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Dining Consumer Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Transparent Stalls — Entry is free; fresh produce, spices, flowers, and freshly shucked Oualidia oysters carry clear displayed or standardized per-kilo prices. *(good)* `Source: Marché Central Commercial Board`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Historic Market — Established in 1917 with neo-Mauresque keyhole arches, serving as Casablanca's primary culinary crossroads for local chefs and residents. *(good)* `Source: Casamémoire Historic Markets Index`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Slippery Concrete — Compact single-level hall with zero stairs, but concrete floors around seafood and ice preparation areas are frequently wet and slick. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Municipal Market Standards`

---

### 7. Église du Sacré-Cœur / Cathedral of Casablanca (id: casablanca-sacree-coeur)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Restored Interior — Fully rehabilitated multi-purpose cultural monument managed by Casa Events & Animation with on-site security, emergency exits, and monitored doors. *(good)* `Source: Casablanca Events & Animation`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Management — State-supervised municipal cultural space reopened in late 2023; completely free from unauthorized ticketing or street touts. *(good)* `Source: Médias24 Cultural Heritage Records`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Nominal Entry — Exterior viewing is free; temporary exhibitions and art fairs hosted inside charge nominal, transparent admission tickets (~20–30 MAD). *(good)* `Source: Espace Sacré-Cœur Official Schedule`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Art Deco — 1930 reinforced-concrete masterpiece designed by Paul Tournon, blending French monumental Art Deco with Moorish openwork screens. *(good)* `Source: Ministère de la Jeunesse, de la Culture et de la Communication`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step-Free Nave — Vast ground-floor exhibition nave is level and fully wheelchair-accessible; accessing upper gallery viewpoints involves steep, narrow interior stairs. *(good)* `Source: Parc de la Ligue Arabe Urban Survey`

---

### 8. Villa des Arts de Casablanca (id: casablanca-villa-des-arts)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Gated Foundation — Enclosed private cultural compound on Boulevard Brahim Roudani with monitored gate security, peaceful gardens, and surveillance. *(good)* `Source: Fondation Al Mada Safety Standards`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Official Foundation — Non-profit contemporary art institution run directly by Fondation Al Mada; free of third-party sales touts or misleading guides. *(good)* `Source: Villa des Arts Administration`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Admission — Completely free public entry to permanent modern collections, temporary exhibitions, and outdoor sculpture gardens. *(good)* `Source: Fondation Al Mada Official Portal`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Contemporary Haven — 1934 Art Deco villa converted into a premier arts venue showcasing contemporary Moroccan painters, sculptors, and multimedia installations. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Contemporary Art Registry`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Interior Staircase — Garden and ground-floor galleries are flat and accessible; reaching the second-story temporary gallery requires climbing a central staircase. *(good)* `Source: Culturama Accessibility Guide`

---

### 9. Museum of Moroccan Judaism / Musée du Judaïsme Marocain (id: casablanca-jewish-museum)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Strictly Guarded — Secure residential setting in Oasis with armed police presence, mandatory checkpoint bag checks, and metal detector screening at the gate. *(good)* `Source: Fondation du Patrimoine Culturel Judéo-Marocain`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Accredited Museum — The Arab world's only dedicated Jewish heritage museum, operated under formal communal foundation governance with zero commercial touting. *(good)* `Source: National Museum Foundation Network`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Tariff — Official entry fee is fixed at 50 MAD per person, payable directly at the front reception counter with receipts provided. *(good)* `Source: Musée du Judaïsme Box Office`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Unique Heritage — Founded in 1997 by Simon Lévy, displaying authentic Moroccan Torah scrolls, bimahs, traditional Berber Jewish jewelry, and reconstructed rural synagogues. *(good)* `Source: UNESCO Cultural Diversity Archives`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Single Story — Compact single-story villa with smooth, level interior tile floors and gentle garden walkways, easily navigable for all mobility levels. *(good)* `Source: Oasis District Cultural Survey`

---

### 10. Église Notre-Dame de Lourdes (id: casablanca-notre-dame-de-lourdes)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Peaceful Sanctuary — Active Catholic parish on Rond-Point d'Europe; calm, respectful interior monitored by church attendants and parish staff. *(good)* `Source: Archidiocèse de Rabat`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Low Risk — Purely religious sanctuary with no entry ticketing; decline self-appointed street parking guardians requesting high fees outside the perimeter gates. *(good)* `Source: Casablanca Parish Visitor Guidelines`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Access — Completely free admission for worship, silent prayer, or admiring the architecture; voluntary collection boxes are strictly optional. *(good)* `Source: Notre-Dame de Lourdes Parish Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Master Glasswork — Modernist concrete sanctuary built in 1954 by Achille Dangleterre, featuring 800 square meters of genuine stained glass by French master Gabriel Loire. *(good)* `Source: Inventaire du Patrimoine Moderne Casablanca`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Ramped Access — Broad paved approach with ground-level step-free ramps and a wide, unobstructed central nave suitable for wheelchairs and strollers. *(good)* `Source: Church Mobility Audit`

---

### 11. Boulevard Mohammed V & Art Deco Architectural Tour (id: casablanca-art-deco-walk)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Urban Traffic — Major commercial boulevard bisected by fast-moving Casa Tramway tracks; pedestrians must watch for motorbikes and keep bags secure from pickpockets. *(caution)* `Source: Casa Transports Safety Reports`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Unaccredited Touts — Street hustlers frequently offer impromptu architectural tours; book verified walking tours through Casamémoire for authentic historical insight. *(caution)* `Source: Casamémoire Guide Network`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Self-Guided — Self-guided exterior street architecture walks are 100% free; structured volunteer-led Casamémoire weekend heritage walks run ~50–100 MAD. *(good)* `Source: Casamémoire Official Tariffs`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Open-Air Museum — The world’s greatest open-air ensemble of 1920s–1930s Art Deco, Streamline Moderne, and Neo-Mauresque architecture (Hôtel Lincoln, Immeuble Maret, Central Post Office). *(good)* `Source: UNESCO World Heritage Tentative Dossier`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Urban Sidewalks — 2 to 3 km of continuous urban walking; sidewalks are mostly level, but broken pavement flags, curbs, and construction works require careful footwork. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Pedestrian Infrastructure Audit`

### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (3)
### 12. La Corniche & Ain Diab Beach (id: casablanca-corniche)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Heavy Undertow — Well-lit pedestrian promenade with regular police patrols; swimming in the ocean is hazardous due to powerful Atlantic undertows and rip currents. *(caution)* `Source: Protection Civile Casablanca`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Parking Touts — Beware of informal "gilets jaunes" parking guardians demanding arbitrary fees and unlicensed operators hawking quad bike rides along the avenue. *(caution)* `Source: Sûreté Nationale Casablanca`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Promenade — Walking the public promenade is 100% free; private beach clubs, oceanfront lounges, and cafés charge premium, clearly posted menu rates. *(good)* `Source: Visit Casablanca Commercial Registry`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Urban Leisure — Casablanca's primary seaside gathering avenue, offering authentic local urban leisure and sunset family strolls rather than traditional crafts. *(good)* `Source: Casablanca Urban Tourism Review`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step-Free Level — Completely flat, wide paved promenade spanning several kilometers, fully accessible for wheelchairs, baby strollers, and relaxed walking. *(good)* `Source: Casa Transports Accessibility Guide`

---

### 13. Ain Diab & Plage Lalla Meryem (id: casablanca-corniche-beaches)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Treacherous Currents — Powerful Atlantic undertows and submerged rocky shelves make swimming hazardous; lifeguards patrol only designated zones during summer months. *(caution)* `Source: Protection Civile Maritime Watch`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Parasol Hawkers — Freelance vendors aggressively push sun loungers, pony rides, and surf rentals with negotiable rates; establish fees firmly beforehand. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Beach Operations Advisory`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Free Shore — Public sand access is free; daily parasol rentals (~30–50 MAD) and local surf club board rentals require upfront price agreement. *(good)* `Source: Commune de Casablanca`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Local Shoreline — Authentic local social scene famous for energetic sunset beach soccer matches, grassroots surf sessions, and local families picnicking by the sea. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Coastal Sports Guild`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Soft Sand — Reached via concrete stairs and ramps from the promenade, with soft deep sand requiring moderate leg effort to walk across. *(good)* `Source: Casablanca Coastal Trail Survey`

---

### 14. Port of Casablanca (id: casablanca-port)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Industrial Traffic — Heavy industrial truck traffic, active rail lines, and restricted customs zones; visitor access is strictly limited to the passenger and fishing docks. *(caution)* `Source: Agence Nationale des Ports (ANP)`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Grill Overcharging — Unofficial taxi drivers solicit cruise arrivals with inflated flat rates; seafood stalls near the docks often inflate weights or tack on unexpected sides. *(caution)* `Source: Casa-Port Transport Police Advisories`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Confirm Upfront — Harbor exterior is free; dining at port fish stalls or taking taxis requires insisting on official scales and taxi meters before service. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Consumer Protection Agency`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Working Seaport — Morocco's largest industrial shipping hub and historic sardine port, retaining genuine maritime trades, active fishing skiffs, and fish markets. *(good)* `Source: Office National des Pêches (ONP)`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Level Slippery — Level terrain throughout, but wet concrete around fishing berths, high curbs, and heavy vehicle traffic make sturdy footwear necessary. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Port Authority Guidelines`

### 🎽 Sport & Active (28)
### 15. Kitesurfing at Ain Diab (id: casablanca-kitesurf-corniche)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Wind Dependent — IKO-certified instructors supply safety release leashes, impact vests, and helmets, but Atlantic shorebreak and shifting winds require vigilance. *(caution)* `Source: IKO Kitesurf Safety Guidelines`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Accredited Schools — Book through established nautical clubs along Ain Diab; avoid informal beach promoters renting gear without rescue support. *(good)* `Source: Fédération Royale Marocaine de Voile`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Structured Rates — Standard 2-hour discovery or beginner kite lessons run 450–600 MAD, fully covering kite, harness, board, and wetsuit. *(good)* `Source: Ain Diab Surf & Nautical Rates`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Atlantic Sport — Genuine Moroccan coastal action sport culture developed around Ain Diab's Atlantic swells and trade winds. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Surfing & Sailing Guild`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** High Exertion — Physically demanding activity requiring core strength, kite control coordination, and solid swimming endurance in open ocean water. *(caution)* `Source: Water Sports Physical Matrix`

---

### 16. Camel Ride on Ain Diab Beach (id: casablanca-camel-ride-corniche)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Led Reins — Handlers lead dromedaries on foot along the tide line; animals can be tall and step abruptly when rising or sitting. *(caution)* `Source: Protection Civile Ain Diab`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Hustler Caution — Independent handlers roaming the sand quote arbitrary tourist prices and demand extra tips; firmly agree on total cost and duration before mounting. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Coastal Police Reports`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Bargaining Needed — Informally priced; expect to pay 50–100 MAD for a 15–20 minute stroll, but assertive negotiation is essential. *(caution)* `Source: Traveler Field Audits`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Tourist Novelty — Primarily an urban commercial tourist photo-op rather than an authentic Saharan caravan experience. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Urban Tourism Review`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Balance — Sedentary ride requiring modest core balance to stay upright as the camel stands and kneels; suitable for all ages. *(good)* `Source: Equine & Camel Tourism Standards`

---

### 17. Harbor & Coast Boat Tour (id: casablanca-boat-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Marine Inspected — Motorized excursion boats operating from the Port of Casablanca equipped with life jackets, radio, and licensed maritime skippers. *(good)* `Source: Direction de la Marine Marchande`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Marina — Book departures directly via the Port/Marina reception or registered tour portals; avoid unauthorized dockside touts. *(good)* `Source: Port de Casablanca Maritime Services`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Excursion — Standard 1.5 to 2-hour coastal boat tours run 250–350 MAD per passenger, clearly covering life vests and fuel. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Casablanca Nautical Desk`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Maritime Panorama — Authentic vantage point viewing Casablanca's skyline, Hassan II Mosque's sea minaret, and industrial container docks. *(good)* `Source: ANP Casablanca Tourism Archive`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Seated Leisure — Relaxed seated boating with zero physical exertion, though Atlantic swells outside the breakwater can cause motion sickness. *(caution)* `Source: Marine Travel Health Advisory`

---

### 18. Quad Bike Desert & Beach Adventure (id: casablanca-quad-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Safety Briefed — Features automatic quad bikes, protective full-face helmets, eyewear, and trail marshals enforcing speed limits. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Adventure Tourism Regulations`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Licensed Operator — Book with registered excursion agencies in Bouskoura or south coast dunes to ensure insurance coverage and well-maintained machinery. *(good)* `Source: Fédération Marocaine des Sports Automobiles`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Packages — Standard 2-hour guided off-road quad excursions cost ~400–550 MAD per machine, with transparent rates for tandem riders. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Off-Road Scenery — Scenic outdoor adventure exploring coastal tracks and rural coastal plains south of Casablanca (Dar Bouazza / Sidi Rahal). *(good)* `Source: Casablanca Hinterland Tourism Review`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Moderate Vibration — Intuitive thumb-throttle controls, but driving across sandy ruts and gravel trails requires arm grip and causes body vibration. *(caution)* `Source: ATV Driving Guidelines`

---

### 19. Corniche Bike Tour (id: casablanca-bike-corniche)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Helmet Provided — Uses geared hybrid or city bikes with safety helmets, guided along dedicated bike paths on the renovated Corniche promenade. *(good)* `Source: Casablanca Urban Mobility Standards`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Booking — Coordinated through recognized rental kiosks or tour agencies; inspect bike brakes and gears before rolling out to avoid dispute. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Standard Rates — Guided 2-hour seaside bike tours cost ~150–200 MAD per rider, while independent bike rentals are ~40–60 MAD per hour. *(good)* `Source: Casa Transports Rental Kiosks`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Urban Coastal — Contemporary leisure ride mingling with local Casaoui cyclists, runners, and families along Ain Diab and the Hassan II Mosque esplanade. *(good)* `Source: Visit Casablanca Official`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Step-Free Flat — Wide, smooth paved bike lanes with zero elevation gain; effortless pedaling suitable for all ages and fitness levels. *(good)* `Source: Corniche Infrastructure Survey`

---

### 20. Deep Sea Fishing Charter (id: casablanca-fishing-charter)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Vessel Equipped — Coast guard-approved offshore fishing boats equipped with life vests, GPS navigation, fish finders, and licensed skippers. *(good)* `Source: Office National des Pêches (ONP)`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Club Coordinated — Book charters directly via Casablanca Yacht Club or licensed offshore charters; confirm target species and trip length in advance. *(good)* `Source: Royal Yacht Club de Casablanca`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Charter Rates — Half-day private or shared offshore sport-fishing charters run 600–900 MAD per person, including rods, bait, tackle, and fuel. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Atlantic Angling — Genuine maritime sport fishing targeting Atlantic sea bass, sea bream, and bonito alongside experienced local skippers. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Sport Fishing Federation`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Motion Sensitive — Low physical exertion while waiting for bites, but deep-sea Atlantic swells can trigger acute seasickness. *(caution)* `Source: Offshore Maritime Health Advisory`

---

### 21. Surf Lesson at Ain Diab (id: casablanca-surf-lesson)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Certified Coaches — Lessons use buoyant foam beginner boards, fitted wetsuits, and certified instructors supervising waist-deep waters. *(good)* `Source: Fédération Royale Marocaine de Surf`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Registered Schools — Avoid uncertified beach boys renting damaged boards on the sand; book with established clubs (e.g. Ain Diab Surf School). *(good)* `Source: Ain Diab Surf Club Registry`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Tariff — Standard group lessons are fixed at 200–250 MAD for a 1.5 to 2-hour session with board, wetsuit, and shower access included. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Club Rates`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Grassroots Surf — Iconic cradle of Moroccan Atlantic surfing, where generations of local surfers have trained on beachbreak waves. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Surf Culture Archive`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Beginner Accessible — Whitewater coaching is designed for first-timers, requiring basic swimming ability and modest paddling stamina. *(good)* `Source: ISA Beginner Coaching Standards`

---

### 22. Horseback Ride on the Corniche (id: casablanca-horse-ride)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Stable Handlers — Accompanied by experienced stable grooms; riding helmets provided, and rides keep to low-tide packed sand. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Equestrian Tourism Association`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Booking — Freelance horse owners on Ain Diab sand charge erratic rates; book through licensed ranches (Dar Bouazza / Ain Diab) to ensure horse welfare. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Police Beach Division`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Agreed Rates — Beach walk rides run 150–250 MAD per hour when booked through recognized clubs; informal beach rides require strict price negotiation. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Field Records`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Equestrian Tradition — Showcases Morocco's storied horsemanship, utilizing noble Berber-Arabian horses along the Atlantic surfline. *(good)* `Source: Société Royale d'Encouragement du Cheval (SOREC)`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Gentle Stride — Calm walking pace along wet sand makes it approachable for beginners and children, with cantering reserved for advanced riders. *(good)* `Source: Equine Tourism Mobility Standards`

---

### 23. Sunset Yacht Cruise (id: casablanca-yacht-sunset)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Safety Inspected — Inspected luxury motor or sailing yachts equipped with certified life vests, radar, emergency beacons, and professional crew. *(good)* `Source: Direction de la Marine Marchande`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Marina Front-Desk — Bookings managed directly through Casablanca Marina docks or accredited concierge services; no dockside ticketing touts. *(good)* `Source: Marina Casablanca Nautical Registry`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Premium Fixed — Sunset yacht excursions cost ~450–700 MAD per person on shared cruises, clearly inclusive of welcome drinks and light refreshments. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Scenic Luxury — High-end nautical experience delivering sunset views over Casablanca’s skyline and the illuminated Hassan II Mosque. *(good)* `Source: Visit Casablanca Maritime Review`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Effortless Seated — Completely passive and relaxing seated voyage requiring zero physical exertion, accessible for all ages. *(good)* `Source: Marine Tourism Accessibility Survey`

---

### 24. Sea Kayak Corniche Tour (id: casablanca-kayak-tour)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Buoyant Vests — Stable sit-on-top ocean kayaks with certified buoyancy life vests, watertight bags, and guide monitoring of coastal chop. *(good)* `Source: Association Nautique Casablanca`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Accredited Kiosks — Book with registered watersports centers near El Hank or Ain Diab; avoid unofficial beach touts lacking safety rescue boats. *(caution)* `Source: Casablanca Coastal Safety Advisories`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Package — 2-hour guided ocean kayak excursion is fixed at ~250–300 MAD per paddler, covering kayak, paddle, life vest, and guide. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / Nautical Club Rates`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Seaside Perspective — Eco-friendly adventure offering up-close views of rocky headlands, El Hank lighthouse, and Atlantic marine life. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Coastal Eco-Tourism`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Upper Body — Requires continuous rhythmic paddling against Atlantic swells and coastal breezes; requires good shoulder stamina and swimming confidence. *(caution)* `Source: Nautical Sports Physical Guidelines`

---

### 25. Desert Quad & Camel Combo (id: casablanca-desert-quad-camel)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Marshaled Activity — Guided combo tour with protective helmets for the quad segment, trained camel handlers, and basic first-aid gear. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Adventure Tour Safety Charter`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Licensed Agency — Book through established excursion agencies to guarantee insured quad machinery and promised activity durations. *(good)* `Source: Casablanca Regional Tourism Delegation`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Combo Tariffs — Combined 3 to 4-hour half-day excursions run 550–750 MAD per person, clearly bundling transport, quad rental, camel trek, and mint tea. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Hybrid Adventure — Staged tourist combo in the coastal dunes/hinterland of Casablanca, blending modern motorsport with traditional camel riding. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Rural Tourism Review`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Moderate Exertion — Quad riding involves trail vibrations and steering effort, followed by a gentle, relaxing seated camel ride. *(caution)* `Source: Adventure Tourism Matrix`

---

### 26. Paragliding over the Coast (id: casablanca-paragliding)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Certified Pilots — Tandem flights with licensed instructors (FRMAL-certified) using dual-harness paragliders, reserve parachutes, and strict wind checks. *(good)* `Source: Fédération Royale Marocaine de l'Aviation Légère`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Booking — Coordinated through registered paragliding clubs operating at certified coastal launch sites; avoid unlicensed operators. *(good)* `Source: Club Parapente Maroc`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Flight — Tandem discovery flights cost ~700–850 MAD per flight (15–20 minutes), often including GoPro video and photo footage. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Tariff Board`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Aerial Panorama — Breathtaking flight over the crashing Atlantic waves, coastal cliffs, and sweeping shoreline of Greater Casablanca. *(good)* `Source: Adventure Air Tourism Morocco`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Brief Running — Passengers require no piloting experience; requires only a short 5-meter running takeoff and a gentle flare landing on the beach. *(good)* `Source: Tandem Flight Standards`

---

### 27. Electric Bike Desert Tour (id: casablanca-bike-desert)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Helmet Provided — High-torque fat-tire electric bikes equipped with disc brakes, protective safety helmets, and guide supervision along rural tracks. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Cycling Safety Protocols`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Direct Booking — Organized through registered eco-adventure providers; pre-ride battery and brake inspections prevent route disruptions. *(good)* `Source: Casablanca Eco-Mobility Guild`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Fixed Tour — 3-hour guided coastal plain / dune e-bike tour is fixed at ~280–350 MAD per rider, including e-bike, helmet, and guide. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate Verified Listings`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Hinterland Ride — Eco-friendly exploration of pastoral countryside trails, eucalyptus forests (Bouskoura), and Atlantic coastal dunes. *(good)* `Source: Greater Casablanca Green Tourism`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Effortless Pedaling — Wide fat tires and powerful electric assist make sand and dirt climbs easy, suitable for most fitness levels. *(good)* `Source: E-Bike Trail Standards`

---

### 28. City Club Casablanca (id: casablanca-city-club)

* **🛡️ Safety:** **Tag:** Modern Machinery — Large multi-level corporate gym network featuring extensive Technogym cardio machines, weight stacks, and group studio classes. *(good)* `Source: City Club Technical Specs`
* **⚠️ Scams:** **Tag:** Subscription Traps — Infamous for aggressive annual contract renewals and bank debit disputes; short-term visitors should strictly pay one-off cash passes. *(caution)* `Source: Moroccan Consumer Protection Forums`
* **🏷️ Pricing:** **Tag:** Budget Day-Pass — Single-entry visitor pass is priced at 50–60 MAD at the counter, granting access to workout floors and scheduled classes. *(good)* `Source: Moroccan Mate / City Club Front Desk`
* **💎 Authenticity:** **Tag:** Corporate Chain — Morocco's largest discount commercial gym franchise; functional, busy fitness space with zero folkloric or artisanal charm. *(good)* `Source: Clubs.ma City Club Directory`
* **⛰️ Difficulty:** **Tag:** Beginner Friendly — Pin-loaded selectorized weight machines with clear diagrams make independent workouts easy without coaching. *(good)* `Source: City Club Fitness Matrix`

## Chefchaouen — 43 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/chefchaouen/` — 17 heritage · 2 nature · 24 sport

### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (17)
### 1. The Blue Medina of Chefchaouen (`chefchaouen-medina-blue-streets`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Slick Cobblestones" — Pedestrianized and safe from traffic, though stone steps and painted walkways become very slippery when wet. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Persistent Touts" — Unofficial guides offer unsolicited directions for money, and touts frequently approach tourists offering illicit cannabis. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Open public streets with no entrance fee, though private decorated doorsteps occasionally request 5–10 MAD photo tips. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Living Heritage" — Genuine 15th-century mountain medina with authentic residential quarters, woven-textile weavers, and local bakeries. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Hilly Steps" — Built along mountain slopes with frequent steep stairs and uneven stones; inaccessible for wheelchairs. *(caution)*

---

### 2. Plaza Uta el-Hammam (`chefchaouen-plaza-uta-el-hammam`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Well Monitored" — Chefchaouen's primary open square with constant foot traffic, excellent evening lighting, and a visible police presence. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Aggressive Hawkers" — Restaurant waiters aggressively push menus onto passersby; watch for unrequested bread or olives added to the bill. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Variable Menus" — Dining is moderately priced, but menus should be checked in advance to confirm beverage and side-dish charges. *(caution)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Tourist Centric" — The historic social heart of the town, though cafe terraces and restaurants now cater predominantly to tourists. *(caution)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Pavement" — Broad, level paved stone plaza easily navigated on foot with minimal inclines. *(good)*

---

### 3. Kasbah Museum of Chefchaouen (`chefchaouen-kasbah-museum`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Steep Stairs" — Well-maintained walled compound, but climbing the Portuguese Tower involves tight, steep stone steps with low historic parapets. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "State Regulated" — Official municipal heritage monument; strictly buy tickets at the interior front desk to avoid touts. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Ticket" — Official government admission set at 60–80 MAD for foreign visitors and 10–30 MAD for Moroccan nationals/residents. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Historic Fortress" — Authentic 1471 defense fortress built by Moulay Ali Ben Rachid, containing historic gardens, prison cells, and regional exhibits. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Tower Climb" — Lower Andalusian gardens and courtyards are flat, but reaching panoramic battlements requires steep stair climbing. *(caution)*

---

### 4. Spanish Mosque Viewpoint (`chefchaouen-spanish-mosque`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Unlit Path" — Safe and busy during sunset hours, but the rocky path has no streetlights and requires a phone light or torch after dark. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Occasional Touts" — Informal musicians and beverage hawkers operate near the summit, along with occasional offers of hashish along the trail. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Public panoramic viewpoint with zero entrance fee; small cash needed only if purchasing fresh orange juice from trailside vendors. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Historic Landmark" — 1920s Spanish protectorate-era structure providing Chefchaouen's quintessential panoramic vista for both locals and travelers. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Moderate Incline" — Continuous 20-to-30 minute uphill walk from Ras El Maa over gravel, dirt, and stone steps. *(caution)*

---

### 5. Grande Mosquée of Chefchaouen (`chefchaouen-grand-mosque`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Well Protected" — Centrally located right on Plaza Uta el-Hammam in a busy, heavily patrolled pedestrian zone. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Restricted Entry" — Active Muslim place of worship closed to non-Muslim visitors; anyone offering paid interior access is fraudulent. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Viewing" — Viewing and photographing the distinctive exterior and octagonal minaret from the public square is free. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Sacred Landmark" — Genuine 15th-century historical sanctuary showcasing northern Moroccan Andalusian-Maghrebi Islamic architecture. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Level Pavement" — Viewable directly from the flat paving of Plaza Uta el-Hammam; fully accessible for prams and wheelchairs. *(good)*

---

### 6. Bab El Onsar Gate (`chefchaouen-bab-el-ansar`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Moist Footing" — Positioned near water channels leading to Ras El Maa; stone paving can become wet and slick. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Faux Guides" — Unofficial guides occasionally position themselves outside the gate to steer tourists into shops or offer paid trail guiding. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Passage" — Historic public stone gateway open 24/7 with no admission fees or tolls. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Historic Portal" — Authentic medieval stone gate built into the eastern ramparts that has linked the medina to mountain springs for centuries. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Stepped Incline" — Features cobblestone stairs and sloping ramps leading down to the riverbed. *(caution)*

---

### 7. Medina Ramparts and Towers (`chefchaouen-medina-ramparts`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Uneven Masonry" — Unrestored outer wall sections lack modern railings and feature loose stones and sheer drops. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Informal Touts" — Local touts near quiet wall perimeters offer to open "private rampart viewpoints" on residential roofs for inflated tips. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Exterior" — Free to inspect along public street perimeters; preserved walkable battlements inside the Kasbah require the Kasbah ticket. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Medieval Defense" — Genuine 15th-to-16th-century stone curtain walls and watchtowers built to defend against Portuguese attacks. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Steep Slopes" — Tracing the perimeter requires ascending and descending steep mountain hillside alleys. *(caution)*

---

### 8. Ethnographic Museum of Chefchaouen (`chefchaouen-ethnographic-museum`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Enclosed Facility" — Secured, quiet municipal museum setting housed in the left wing of the Kasbah fortress. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "State Regulated" — Managed under the Ministry of Youth, Culture and Communication; tickets are only sold at the official gate counter. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Ticket" — Official government admission (60–80 MAD foreign tourists, 10–30 MAD Moroccans/residents) covers both the museum and kasbah. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Tribal Heritage" — Curated collections of authentic Jebala regional bridal attire, folk musical instruments, pottery, and antique weapons. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Stair Climbing" — Historic two-story converted palatial building with stone steps between display rooms; no elevator access. *(caution)*

---

### 9. Chefchaouen Souk District (`chefchaouen-souk`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Dense Corridors" — Narrow, crowded market thoroughfares; keep valuables zipped to guard against opportunistic pickpocketing. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Bargaining Pressure" — Mass-produced souvenirs are often billed as hand-crafted Riffian items; hashish touts frequent side branches. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Negotiable Rates" — Souvenirs, brassware, and carpets require haggling; local fresh fruits, bread, and spices carry low, fixed local prices. *(caution)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Mixed Market" — A genuine community market serving locals with regional goat cheese and woven wool (*mendil*), interspersed with souvenir stalls. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Stepped Alleys" — Irregular cobblestone steps and moderate slopes along all market pathways; entirely pedestrian. *(caution)*

---

### 10. Chefchaouen Artisans & Tannery District (`chefchaouen-tannerie-district`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Wet Surfaces" — Active craft area with exposed natural dye run-off, open drainage, and wet flagstones. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Tipping Pressure" — Unofficial guides offer "behind-the-scenes" workshop tours, then demand large tips or pressure tourists into buying rugs. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Exploration" — Free to walk through public workshop streets; small tips (5–10 MAD) are polite if photographing artisans at work. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Active Craft" — Generational Jbala craftsmen actively weaving wool rugs on pedal looms and practicing indigo dyeing and leatherwork. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Narrow Steps" — Navigated via cramped stone steps and sloping medina lanes; unsuitable for wheeled luggage or wheelchairs. *(caution)*

---

### 11. Avenue Hassan II Promenade (`chefchaouen-uta-hammam-promenade`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Well Lit" — Broad, brightly illuminated modern avenue connecting the new town to the medina, safe late into the night. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Minimal Risk" — Traditional retail and modern administrative zone largely free of faux guides or pushy street hustlers. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Prices" — Cafes, bakeries, and ice cream shops display printed menus with standard local prices in dirhams. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Local Promenade" — Authentic social corridor where Chefchaouen families and residents gather for their evening stroll (*paseo*). *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Gradient" — Smooth paved sidewalks with gentle gradients; fully accessible for strollers and wheelchairs. *(good)*

---

### 12. Place El Haouta (`chefchaouen-place-el-haouta`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Calm Square" — Peaceful residential plaza in the Souika quarter with negligible crime and a low-key atmosphere. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Low Pressure" — Quiet neighborhood square with almost none of the aggressive touting found in main commercial plazas. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Local Pricing" — Mint tea and small meals at the perimeter cafes cost regular medina rates (10–15 MAD) with transparent bills. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Intact Plaza" — Classical Andalusian-style square centered on an active tiled fountain where locals still draw mountain spring water. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Center" — Level paved square once reached, though getting here requires walking along sloping medina streets. *(good)*

### 13. Ras El Maa Historic Springs & Washhouses (`chefchaouen-ras-el-maa-springs`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Slippery Rocks" — Mountain water flows over exposed riverbed stones and wet stairs; wear grippy footwear. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Photo Touts" — Animal handlers (peacocks/monkeys) and women offering henna solicit photos, demanding unexpected fees after the fact. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Public natural spring and historical riverside area with zero admission charges. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Living Washhouse" — Genuine historical spring where local women still wash laundry and wool carpets in flowing stone channels. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Uneven Flagstones" — Wet stone stairs, cobblestone ramps, and small footbridges with irregular surfaces. *(caution)*

- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Public" — Public medina stairway free to walk through, though small discretionary change is useful for photo spots. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Staged Aesthetic" — An authentic residential alley that has been specifically styled and colorfully potted to cater to viral tourism. *(caution)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Stairway Ascent" — Continuous flight of narrow, steep stone stairs climbing through a residential alley. *(caution)*

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Faux Guides" — Unlicensed guides congregate outside the arch to solicit arriving travelers for hotel referrals or tours. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Passage" — Open municipal gateway connecting the colonial town to the medina; no tolls or fees. *(good)*

- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Moderate Trek" — 3 to 4 hours round-trip over uneven rocks, damp paths, and exposed tree roots; not accessible for strollers or wheelchairs. *(caution)*

- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — No admission charge for this natural landmark; private guided day trips from Chefchaouen typically cost 150–200 MAD per person. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Geological Wonder" — Genuine 25-meter-high reddish limestone arch carved by nature over the Oued Farda canyon. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Strenuous Scramble" — 2 to 3 hours round-trip with steep climbs and loose stones; requires a sure foot and moderate hiking fitness. *(caution)*

### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (8)
### 1. Dakhla Oyster Farms (`dakhla-oyster-farms`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Tidal Walkway" — Lagoon-edge oyster pens and wooden walkways; take care on wet planking and rocky shoreline at high tide. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Direct Counter" — Established aquaculture outfits (such as Talhamar) with direct service and no tour intermediary middlemen. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Clear Pricing" — Fresh oysters and seafood tagines sold at transparent blackboard prices (~5–8 MAD per oyster, ~60–80 MAD per tagine). *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Working Farm" — Genuine commercial oyster farming facility operating directly inside the nutrient-rich Dakhla lagoon. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Footing" — Flat ground, easy vehicle access, and a short walk from the parking area to the dining terraces. *(good)*
