### 32. White Dune 4x4 Desert Tour (`dakhla-white-dune-4x4`) ★

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Professional Driver" — Experienced desert 4x4 drivers navigate deep sand, tidal flats, and dunes in licensed passenger vehicles. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Vetted Itinerary" — Book through licensed tourist transport agencies to guarantee agreed stops (White Dune, Asmaa Spring, oyster tasting). *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Set Excursion" — Fixed price of 450 MAD per person (or ~1,000–1,500 MAD per private vehicle) for a 6-hour half-day desert tour. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Iconic Landscape" — Breathtaking natural white sand dune cascading directly into the emerald lagoon, accompanied by wild flamingos. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Light Dune-Walk" — Mostly seated in an air-conditioned 4x4, with a short, moderately steep 5-minute sand climb to the dune summit. *(good)*

---

### 33. Imlili Secret Oasis 4x4 Trek (`dakhla-imlili-oasis-tour`) ★

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Remote Wilderness" — Deep desert expedition (~100 km south) over unpaved Saharan tracks; licensed guide and 4x4 convoy essential. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Licensed Guide" — Book strictly with accredited tour operators to ensure a vetted driver, safety supplies, and the full promised route. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — 600 MAD per person for a full-day (8-hour) private or small-group tour including 4x4 transport, guide, and picnic lunch. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Prehistoric Phenomenon" — Over 160 permanent hypersaline waterholes in a dry salt desert, home to tiny fish that give natural foot pedicures. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Stroll" — The full day is predominantly vehicle touring; walking around the salt pans is flat and effortless. *(good)*

## El Jadida — 23 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/el_jadida/` — 12 heritage · 11 nature

### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (12)
### 1. Cité Portugaise of Mazagan (`el-jadida-cit-portugaise-of-mazagan`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Paved Fortress" — Walled pedestrian historic quarter with calm streets and police presence; stones near the sea gates can be damp and slick. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Informal Touts" — Unofficial guides loiter by Bab el-Bahr (Sea Gate) offering unsolicited tours or rooftop shortcuts for inflated tips. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Strolling the citadel streets, residential lanes, and public squares is completely free. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "UNESCO Heritage" — Authentic 16th-century fortified Portuguese enclave (Mazagan) founded in 1514, showcasing Renaissance military architecture. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Cobblestones" — Mostly flat stone alleys with slight inclines toward the battlements; easy walking for all ages. *(good)*

---

### 2. Portuguese Cistern (Citerne Portugaise) (`el-jadida-portuguese-cistern`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Damp Flooring" — Subterranean vaulted hall with shallow reflective water; stone steps and walkways can be damp and dimly lit. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Official Counter" — Managed by the Ministry of Culture with an on-site ticket window; avoid touts claiming special entrance privileges. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Ticket" — Standard government admission of 70 MAD for international visitors and 10–20 MAD for Moroccan citizens/residents. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Gothic Vaults" — Built in 1514 as an armory/cistern, featuring 25 Manueline stone columns and the famous central light oculus. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Short Stairs" — Requires descending a stone staircase into the subterranean vault; flat inside, but not wheelchair accessible. *(caution)*

---

### 3. Mazagan Ramparts Walk (`el-jadida-mazagan-ramparts-walk`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Low Parapets" — Elevated fortress walkways and bastions have low stone parapets and sheer seaward drops; supervise children closely. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Direct Access" — Freely accessible from public citadel stairs; ignore informal touts demanding fees to access the ramparts. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Walk" — Walking along the public perimeter battlements and the St. Sebastian bastion is free. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "16th Century" — Genuine 1514 Portuguese fortifications and artillery bastions offering uninterrupted panoramas over the Atlantic and harbor. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Stairs Incline" — Involves walking up stone stairs and inclined masonry ramps; level and breezy once atop the rampart path. *(caution)*

---

### 4. Church of the Assumption (Igreja de Nossa Senhora da Assunção) (`el-jadida-church-of-the-assumption`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Plaza Setting" — Located on Place Al-Khattabi in the central citadel; secure, open public pedestrian space. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Exterior Free" — Viewing the facade and Gothic portal from the square is free; ignore street touts offering paid interior keys. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Viewing" — Free facade viewing; interior access is free during designated cultural and art exhibitions. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Manueline Gothic" — 16th-century Catholic parish church featuring an authentic Manueline stone portal and Gothic bell tower. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Level Plaza" — Completely level stone square with step-free viewing; accessible for wheelchairs and strollers. *(good)*

---

### 5. Mohamed Afifi Theatre & Square (`el-jadida-mohamed-afifi-theatre-square`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Well Patrolled" — Central downtown square on Avenue Mohamed VI; brightly lit in the evening with steady family foot traffic. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Civic Space" — Open municipal public space with a formal municipal box office; free from tourist hustlers. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Plaza" — Relaxing in the square is free; theatrical tickets inside the theater carry low municipal rates (20–50 MAD). *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Art Deco" — Landmark 1920s Art Deco municipal theater named after dramatist Mohamed Afifi, anchoring local civic arts. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Paved Plateau" — Wide, flat paved plaza with gentle curbed ramps, easily navigated by all mobility levels. *(good)*

---

### 6. Parc Hassan II (Parc Spiny) (`el-jadida-parc-hassan-ii`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Fenced Park" — Enclosed botanical municipal park maintained by city staff; safe for family recreation and daytime walks. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Public Garden" — Municipal public park completely free from commercial tourist touts and aggressive vendors. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Admission" — Open to the public with free admission; zero entrance fees or ticketing. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Botanical Heritage" — 4.5-hectare coastal green space established in the early 20th century, home to century-old Norfolk Island pines and palms. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Level Paths" — Flat, shaded gravel and paved paths throughout; fully accessible for prams, children, and wheelchairs. *(good)*

### 7. Portuguese City Mosque & Pentagonal Minaret (Al Masjid Al Atiq) (`el-jadida-portuguese-city-mosque-exterior`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Open Street" — Positioned on Rue Hachmi Bahbah; clean, safe, well-illuminated pedestrian thoroughfare. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Restricted Access" — Active place of worship closed to non-Muslim visitors; touts promising paid interior tours are illegitimate. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Exterior" — Viewing and photographing the unique pentagonal minaret from the public square is free. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Converted Tower" — 19th-century mosque uniquely integrated into a 1514 Portuguese defense tower, creating an irregular 5-sided minaret. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Cobblestone" — Easily observed from the flat stone street; accessible for wheelchairs and strollers. *(good)*

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "State Regulated" — Operated under Morocco's High Commission for Former Resistance Members; official institution with no commercial traps. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Admission" — Completely free admission for all visitors; visitor guestbook available for voluntary entries. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Patriotic Archive" — Authentic historical memorial housing anti-colonial resistance archives, underground radio relics, and liberation photos. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Ground Floor" — Level ground-floor access with wide tiled exhibition halls and step-free navigation. *(good)*

---

### 9. Azemmour Medina Day Trip (`el-jadida-azemmour-medina-day-trip`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Peaceful Medina" — Calm, uncrowded riverbank town along the Oum Er-Rbia; very relaxed atmosphere with minimal traffic. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Low Hassle" — Genuine artistic town largely bypassed by mass tourism; local residents are welcoming and faux guides are rare. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Low Budget" — Free to walk the medina; grand shared taxis from El Jadida cost only 8–10 MAD per seat each way. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Living Murals" — Ancient Portuguese ramparts and whitewashed Jewish Mellah alleys decorated with murals by local and international artists. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Walking" — Compact, gentle walking tour over stone alleys and riverside paths with minimal incline. *(good)*

### 10. Phare de Sidi Bouafi (Sidi Bouafi Lighthouse) (`el-jadida-phare-de-sidi-bouafi`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Tower Stairs" — Secure fenced grounds; climbing the tower interior requires ascending a steep, narrow granite spiral staircase. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Official Guard" — Active maritime beacon operated by port authorities; small gratuity to the lighthouse keeper is discretionary. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Discretionary Tip" — Free to view from the public perimeter; entering and ascending the tower typically involves a modest tip (10–20 MAD). *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Historic Beacon" — Authentic 46-meter granite lighthouse constructed in 1916 by WWI German prisoners, featuring an active rotating Fresnel lens. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Steep Spiral" — Ground viewing is flat; reaching the observation gallery demands climbing 248 spiral granite steps. *(caution)*

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Municipal Gallery" — Official city-managed art space with clear public opening hours (Tuesday–Sunday) and no sales pressure. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Entry" — Public admission to the gallery and temporary contemporary art exhibitions is completely free. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Naive Art" — Dedicated tribute to legendary self-taught painter Chaïbia Talal, exhibiting contemporary Moroccan painters beneath 16th-century stone vaults. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Stone Threshold" — Ground-floor stone hall with a small threshold ramp; mostly flat stone flooring accessible to most visitors. *(good)*

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Modest Sanctuary" — Active religious zaouia; decline self-appointed caretakers demanding large fees for viewing the exterior ruins. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Exploring the medieval ruins, minarets, and coastal perimeter is free; small coin donations customary inside the shrine. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Almohad Fortress" — 11th-to-12th-century medieval coastal ribat (*Tit-n-Ftr*) and home of Morocco's largest annual equestrian Tbourida moussem. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Uneven Terrain" — Packed dirt, stone rubble, and sandy pathways around the archaeological site; wear flat, sturdy walking shoes. *(caution)*
### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (11)
### 13. Porte de la Mer (`el-jadida-porte-de-la-mer`)

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Informal Touts" — Unofficial guides linger near the archway offering unsolicited tours or paid shortcuts through private alleys. *(caution)*

- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Evacuation Portal" — Genuine 16th-century Portuguese sea gate where the final garrison evacuated Mazagan for Brazil in 1769. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Ramp" — Slightly sloping cobblestone ramp with minor steps leading through the archway to the port quay. *(good)*

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Rental Hawkers" — Umbrella and sun lounger operators frequently quote inflated rates; confirm equipment prices before sitting. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Beach" — Open public municipal beach; parasol and plastic chair sets cost a modest 30–50 MAD for the day. *(good)*

- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Sand" — Broad, gently sloping expanse of soft sand with direct level access points from the paved corniche. *(good)*

### 15. Sidi Bouzid Beach (`el-jadida-sidi-bouzid-beach`)

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Parking Touts" — Informal parking attendants ("gilets jaunes") actively demand 5–10 MAD along the clifftop corniche. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Shoreline" — Free public beach; shared grand taxis from central El Jadida cost only 5–7 MAD per passenger. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Surfer Haven" — Iconic crescent bay famous for consistent Atlantic surf waves, fresh sea urchin vendors, and local seafood. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Stair Access" — Reaching the sandy cove from the elevated boulevard requires walking down paved ramps or stone stairways. *(good)*

- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Ramped Access" — Reached via a broad, inclined stone ramp from the medina; flat cobbled walking along the top platform. *(good)*

### 2. Sqala du Port (Port Bastion & Defensive Tower) (`essaouira-sqala-port`)

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Official Counter" — Monument managed by the Ministry of Culture; buy admission strictly at the front gatehouse ticket desk. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Ticket" — Standard government admission fee (50 MAD foreign visitors, 10–20 MAD Moroccans/residents) with printed receipts. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Vauban Fortress" — Monumental 1769 defensive bastion (Bab El-Marsa gateway) framing authentic views over the blue fishing fleet and Purpuraires Islands. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Steep Stairs" — Requires climbing steep stone stairways and a spiral tower turret; not accessible for wheelchair users. *(caution)*

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Reputable Gallery" — Established in 1988 as Morocco's pioneer contemporary gallery; transparent artwork provenance with zero street touting. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Admission" — Open to the public with completely free entry; authentic original paintings and thuya wood sculptures carry cataloged prices. *(good)*

- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Dedicated Caretaker" — Maintained by verified heritage trustees; ignore street guides outside claiming they hold the only key. *(good)*

### 14. Bab Doukkala (Monumental Gate & Market Quarter) (`essaouira-bab-doukkala`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Dense Crowds" — Bustling working-class market thoroughfare; watch personal bags for pickpockets during late afternoon shopping peaks. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Minimal Scams" — Traditional residential bazaar geared toward local residents; straightforward transactions with little tourist hassle. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Local Rates" — Authentic neighborhood pricing for seasonal produce, fresh sardines, spices, and everyday household necessities. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "True Neighborhood" — The least commercialized sector of the medina, showcasing unvarnished everyday Essaouiran family and culinary life. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Level Stroll" — Flat paved avenue leading through the monumental stone archway; pedestrian-only, though barrows and carts cause slow foot traffic. *(good)*
### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (8)
### 15. Sidi Kaouki Beach (Plage de Sidi Kaouki) (`essaouira-sidi-kaouki`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Strong Currents" — Open Atlantic beach subject to powerful swells and rip currents; lifeguards are stationed only during peak summer months. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Negotiate Rides" — Beach camel and horse handlers frequently quote inflated prices; agree firmly on ride duration and cost before mounting. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Shoreline" — Free public beach; local bus #2 from Essaouira costs ~7 MAD, and surfboard day-rentals run ~50–100 MAD. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Surfer Haven" — Laid-back Berber coastal village anchored by the whitewashed Marabout shrine of Sufi saint Sidi Kaouki right on the rocks. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Sand" — Wide, level expanse of sand with easy access from village cafes; walking along loose dry sand requires mild effort. *(good)*

---

### 16. Essaouira Beach (Plage d'Essaouira) (`essaouira-beach`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Sandblasting Winds" — Sheltered bay with gentle waves and summer lifeguards, though fierce trade winds (*Alizées*) cause frequent afternoon sandstorms. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Animal Touts" — Informal quad, camel, and horse handlers along the southern shore aggressively solicit rides; fix all prices beforehand. *(caution)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Free Public" — Completely free municipal beach; sunbed and umbrella sets rent for ~30–50 MAD, and beach clubs publish fixed rates. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Local Seaside" — Vibrant town beach where local Essaouirans play evening football matches alongside windsurfers and families strolling the shore. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Level Boardwalk" — Broad, flat sandy shoreline with direct paved ramp access from Boulevard Mohammed VI; fully accessible for all ages. *(good)*
### 🌿 Wellness & Living Arts (3)
### 23. L'Atelier Essaouira (Cooking Class) (`essaouira-cooking-class`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Sanitized Stations" — Clean, modern culinary studio in a converted warehouse with dedicated cooking stations, supervised burners, and strict food hygiene. *(good)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Established Studio" — Operating since 2017 with verified online bookings, confirmed itineraries, and no unexpected third-party middlemen. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "All Inclusive" — Fixed upfront tuition (~600 MAD / ~€60 per person) covering guided souk shopping, all ingredients, mint tea, and a full 2-course meal. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Hands-On Local" — Sourcing spices directly in the local medina souk followed by hands-on crafting of traditional tagines, pastillas, or couscous. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Standing" — Involves a leisurely 45-minute flat souk walk, followed by comfortable standing and seated food preparation. *(good)*

---

### 24. Hammam Mounia / the Two Doors (`essaouira-hammam-mounia`)

- **🛡️ Safety:** Tag "Slick Tiling" — Steamy, wet marble and tile floors can be slippery underfoot; sanitized private rooms with assisted care from staff. *(caution)*
- **⚠️ Scams:** Tag "Direct Booking" — Well-regarded medina bathhouse operating since 2001 with transparent reservations, designated private slots, and no street touts. *(good)*
- **🏷️ Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Menu" — Transparent treatment menu starting from ~250 MAD (€25) for traditional scrubs up to ~450–600 MAD for packages with argan oil massage. *(good)*
- **💎 Authenticity:** Tag "Ancestral Ritual" — Traditional five-step Moroccan cleansing ritual utilizing authentic black soap, kessa glove exfoliation, ghassoul clay, and pure argan oil. *(good)*
- **⛰️ Difficulty:** Tag "Steam Heat" — Involves sustained warm steam room exposure, firm full-body exfoliating scrubs, and reclining on warm stone benches. *(caution)*

## Fes — 25 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/fes/` — 22 heritage · 3 wellness

### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (22)
### 1. Chouara Tannery (id: `fes-chouara-tannery`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Slippery Walkway" — Open stone terraces and viewing balconies lack safety nets; floors can be slick with organic runoff. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Aggressive Hustlers" — Unofficial guides offer "free" balcony entry or mint sprigs, then demand aggressive tips or high-pressure shop sales. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Tip Dependent" — No official entrance ticket; shops expect a 10–20 MAD tip per person or a leather purchase to access viewing terraces. (caution)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Centuries-Old Tradition" — Functioning medieval leather tannery operating with authentic manual labor, pigeon-guano vats, and natural dyes. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Stair Heavy" — Reaching the best viewing terraces requires ascending 3 to 4 flights of narrow, steep, unrailed stone staircases. (caution)

---

### 2. Al-Attarine Madrasa (id: `fes-al-attarine-madrasa`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Well Maintained" — Solid historical structure managed by the Ministry of Culture; well-lit open courtyard and guarded entrance. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Official Gate" — Entry tickets are sold at a fixed official desk, minimizing tout interaction inside the monument. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Standard official admission ticket is 20 MAD per adult at the counter. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Masterpiece Marinid" — Exceptional 14th-century architectural monument retaining original cedar carving, intricate zellige, and carved stucco. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Mostly Flat" — Main courtyard is fully level marble; upper student quarters require climbing narrow, uneven historical stairs. (good)

---

### 3. Bou Inania Madrasa (id: `fes-bou-inania-madrasa`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Safe Heritage" — Active municipal heritage site with security staff and municipal maintenance; non-Muslims restricted during prayer hours. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Touting Free" — Official ticket counter at the entrance eliminates pricing disputes; ignore touts outside claiming it is closed. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Official admission fee is 20 MAD per person paid directly at the entrance desk. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Living Landmark" — Rare Marinid madrasa that still functions as an active Friday congregational mosque with authentic Marinid decor. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Easy Access" — Ground-floor central courtyard and ablution water channel are flat and step-free from the main alleyway. (good)

---

### 4. Bab Bou Jeloud (The Blue Gate) (id: `fes-bab-bou-jeloud`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Heavily Patrolled" — Constant tourist police (Brigade Touristique) presence and high pedestrian traffic keep the gate area secure day and night. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Touts Hub" — High concentration of fake guides offering unsolicited medina tours or falsely claiming routes are closed. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Public Free" — Public city gate with completely free 24/7 access. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Colonial Historic" — Built in 1913 under the French protectorate in classical Moorish style; serves as the medina's genuine daily artery. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Gradual Incline" — Paved, gently sloping thoroughfare suitable for all fitness levels; medina floor beyond transitions to cobblestones. (good)

---

### 5. Dar al-Makhzen (Royal Palace of Fes) (id: `fes-royal-palace`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Heavily Guarded" — Royal Moroccan Gendarmerie and palace guards secure the perimeter; photographing guards or perimeter walls is strictly forbidden. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Low Risk" — Guards prevent unofficial loitering in the square; minor unsolicited photographers offering paid instant photos. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Viewing" — Public esplanade (Place des Alaouites) with zero entrance fee; interior is strictly closed to the general public. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Active Palace" — Working royal residence showcasing authentic modern Moroccan brass craftsmanship and carved cedar eaves. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Paved Flat" — Vast, completely level pedestrian plaza with smooth paving; universally accessible. (good)

---

### 6. Marinid Tombs (id: `fes-marinid-tombs`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Isolated Twilight" — Daytime visits are secure, but the hill is unlit at dusk; lone travelers face risks of bag snatching or mugging after sunset. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Unofficial Parking" — Men in reflective vests may demand informal parking fees (10–20 MAD) or attempt pushy ad-hoc tour guiding. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Entry" — Open archaeological hillside with no ticket booth or admission charge. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Ancient Ruins" — Weathered, unvarnished 14th-century Marinid royal necropolis overlooking the medina basin. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Steep Climb" — Reaching on foot from the medina involves a steep 15–20 minute uphill walk on uneven dirt trails; accessible easily by taxi. (caution)

---

### 7. Ibn Danan Synagogue (id: `fes-ibn-danan-synagogue`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Secure Mellah" — Quiet street in the historic Jewish Quarter with a resident caretaker monitoring visitor entries. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Informal Guides" — Street kids occasionally shadow tourists to the doorway demanding tips; deal exclusively with the caretaker inside. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Caretaker Fee" — Modest entrance fee/donation of 20–30 MAD collected on-site for historic site upkeep and preservation. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Preserved Heritage" — Restored 17th-century community synagogue containing the original wooden bimah, Torah ark, and underground mikveh. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Narrow Stairs" — Accessing the subterranean ritual bath (mikveh) and roof terrace requires maneuvering extremely steep, narrow steps. (caution)

---

### 8. Nejjarine Museum of Wooden Arts & Crafts (id: `fes-nejjarine-museum`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Secure Facility" — Fully restored fondouk managed by the Mohammed Karim Lamrani Foundation with museum attendants on every floor. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Regulated Entry" — No touting permitted inside; official reception desk with printed admission receipts. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Admission is 20 MAD per adult; optional rooftop tea café charges clearly listed drink prices. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Protected Fondouk" — UNESCO-restored 18th-century caravansary presenting genuine regional woodwork, tools, and carpentry heritage. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Stair Climb" — Three gallery floors accessed via tiled stairwells; rooftop terrace requires additional stair climbing (no elevator). (caution)

---

### 9. Zaouia Moulay Idriss II (id: `fes-zaouia-moulay-idriss`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Crowded Sacred" — High pedestrian volume in narrow alleys; keep valuables secure from opportunistic pickpocketing near the entrance. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Touts Outside" — Street vendors and fake guides congregate near the wooden lintel barriers offering unwanted religious guides. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Exterior" — No charge to view exterior portals; interior prayer hall is strictly restricted to Muslims only. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Living Sanctuary" — One of the holiest pilgrimage sites in Morocco, retaining deep religious devotion and traditional brass gift-slots. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Threshold Step" — Surrounding alleyways feature low wooden beam barriers (horm); walking surface is flat medina cobblestone. (good)

---

### 10. University of al-Qarawiyyin (Exterior) (id: `fes-al-qarawiyyin`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Heavily Trafficked" — Main commercial artery with constant pedestrian footfall; watch personal bags in dense crowds. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Entry Promises" — Aggressive touts claim they can smuggle non-Muslims inside the mosque for money; do not pay them, as access is strictly barred. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Exterior" — Free to peer through open doorways and admire the carved courtyard from street thresholds. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Historic Core" — Oldest continually operating university in the world (founded 859 AD); remains an active spiritual and scholarly institution. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Pavements" — Flat surrounding stone alleys; viewing requires only standing at threshold doorways. (good)

---

### 11. Traditional Moroccan Cooking Class at Cafe Clock (id: `fes-cafe-clock`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Hygienic Kitchen" — Professional culinary workshop following food-safe prep standards and guided market ingredient sourcing. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Reputable Business" — Well-established cross-cultural institution with transparent pre-booking and no unagreed stops. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Transparent Menu" — Approximately 600 MAD for full cooking classes (baking workshops ~400 MAD); all market ingredients and meals included. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Hands-On Local" — Taught by local Moroccan chefs, starting with a guided neighborhood souk shopping trip and communal baking. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Light Standing" — Involves 1–2 hours on foot through local food souks followed by standing prep at kitchen counters. (good)

---

### 12. Place Seffarine (id: `fes-place-seffarine`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Noisy Square" — Safe open square; beware of flying sparks or flying metal dust if standing directly adjacent to open workshops. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Souvenir Pressure" — Surrounding brassware shops apply moderate pressure on browsing tourists; negotiate firmly on copper pieces. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Public artisan plaza with zero admission costs to watch coppersmiths work. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Generational Metalcraft" — Working craftsman square where coppersmiths hand-hammer brass pots, buckets, and platters daily. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Cobblestones" — Compact, level stone square shaded by a central plane tree; easily walkable. (good)

---

### 13. Rainbow Street (id: `fes-rainbow-street`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Tight Corridor" — Extremely narrow lane off Talaa Sghira; keep backpacks in front to avoid pickpockets during crowded passages. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Photo Demands" — Resident artists and shopkeepers may vocally demand tips or small purchases if you take detailed photos of their displays. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Passage" — Public thoroughfare; buying postcards or leaving 5–10 MAD is customary if taking dedicated photos. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Commercial Art" — A photogenic art installation created by local artisans to attract foot traffic and sell paintings; more tourist-focused than historic. (caution)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Steps" — Cobbled pathway featuring several short, brightly painted steps and ramps. (good)

---

### 14. Borj Nord Arms Museum (id: `fes-borj-nord`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Guarded Fortress" — Enclosed military museum with security guards and maintained stone parapets. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Official Gate" — Formal ticket counter run by the preservation agency; no unofficial guides operating inside. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Official admission fee is 20 MAD per adult (discounts for students/groups). (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Saadian Fort" — Genuine 16th-century northern bastion built by Sultan Ahmed al-Mansur, housing historic weaponry from diverse eras. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Fortress Steps" — Exploring interior halls and reaching the cannon ramparts requires ascending uneven stone steps. (caution)

---

### 15. Jewish Cemetery of Fes (id: `fes-jewish-cemetery`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Enclosed Sanctuary" — Walled and locked grounds monitored by an on-site caretaker and security door. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Pushy Guidance" — Caretakers or self-appointed attendants may follow visitors narrating tomb histories and expecting large tips. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Modest Donation" — Informal entry fee/donation of 10–20 MAD paid at the front gate for site maintenance. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Sacred Ground" — Revered centuries-old burial ground featuring distinct whitewashed barrel-vaulted tombs and rabbis' shrines. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Uneven Paths" — Narrow, uneven paved walkways between dense tombs; gentle hill slope requires sturdy footwear. (good)

---

### 16. Palais El Glaoui (id: `fes-palais-el-glaoui`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Decaying Structure" — Portions of the historic palace are structurally neglected; upper floors are closed for safety. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Alley Misdirection" — Touts along outer streets regularly tell visitors "it is closed" to divert them to other shops. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Caretaker Fee" — Entry fee of 25 MAD per person paid directly in cash to the caretaker/resident family. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Faded Grandeur" — Hauntingly authentic early 20th-century Glaoui family palace left in an evocative semi-ruined state. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Level Ground" — Ground-floor courtyards and salon rooms are flat, though floors have crumbling tile patches. (good)

---

### 17. Musée Al Batha des Arts de l'Islam (id: `fes-batha-museum`)
- **Safety:** Tag "State Museum" — Newly renovated national museum under the Fondation Nationale des Musées; modern lighting and security protocols. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Official Only" — Professional ticketing desk and regulated entry, preventing any medina guide schemes. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Standard entry is 60 MAD per adult (free on Fridays for Moroccan citizens). (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Royal Collection" — Former 19th-century royal palace displaying over 6,500 masterworks of authentic Moroccan ceramic, wood, and Islamic arts. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Easy Garden" — Beautiful flat Andalusian courtyard gardens and ramps, making it one of the medina's most accessible cultural sites. (good)

---

### 18. Sahrij Madrasa (id: `fes-sahrij-madrasa`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Quiet Quarter" — Located in the less crowded Andalusian quarter; calm, unhurried, and monitored by an attendant. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Low Pressure" — Far fewer touts compared to central madrasas; genuine ticket counter at the entrance. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Official admission fee is 20 MAD per adult. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Intact Marinid" — Completed in 1321, featuring authentic carved cedar beams, calligraphic friezes, and its signature large central water basin. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Central" — Central pool courtyard is completely flat; upper student dormitory rooms require climbing narrow stone stairs. (good)

---

### 19. Jardin Jnan Sbil (Bou Jeloud Gardens) (id: `fes-jnan-sbil`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Patrolled Parkland" — Enclosed municipal park patrolled by park wardens; gates are strictly locked at dusk. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "No Touts" — Free public green space completely removed from medina hustlers and commercial vendors. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Entry" — 100% free municipal botanical garden open daily except Mondays. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Historic Gardens" — 19th-century royal gardens commissioned by Sultan Moulay Hassan I, featuring heritage waterwheels, palms, and citrus groves. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Pavements" — Wide, flat paved alleys and gravel trails; fully accessible for wheelchairs, strollers, and all ages. (good)

---

### 20. Cherratine Madrasa (id: `fes-cherratine-madrasa`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Quiet Compound" — Calm heritage monument supervised by an on-site gatekeeper; minimal tourist congestion. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Direct Desk" — Tickets are issued directly at the entrance doorway without tout interference. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Official admission fee is 20 MAD per visitor. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Alawite Heritage" — Built in 1670 by Sultan Moulay al-Rashid; features authentic functional student quarters over three distinct stories. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Multi-Tier Stairs" — Central courtyard is flat, but reaching the dormitories and upper verandas requires walking up steep stairwells. (caution)

---

### 21. Borj Sud (South Bastion) (id: `fes-borj-sud`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Isolated Hilltop" — Unmanned overlook; walking the isolated footpath up from Bab Rcif alone at dusk is strongly discouraged due to mugging risks. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Taxi Demands" — Petit taxi drivers often refuse the meter for this hilltop trip; agree on a fixed round-trip wait fare before departing. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Panorama" — No fee to access the open panoramic viewpoints outside the fortress walls. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Saadian Fortress" — Original 16th-century fortress (military-occupied, exterior only) offering an authentic commanding view of Fes el-Bali. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Hilltop Road" — Hillside location; arriving on foot is a steep, strenuous uphill trek, but stepping out of a taxi at the top is completely flat. (caution)

---

### 22. Ain Nokbi Artisan Pottery & Zellige Village (id: `fes-pottery-ain-nokbi`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Industrial Hazards" — Active production complex with hot open-flame kilns, dust, sharp ceramic shards, and uneven floors. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Hard Sell" — Guided workshop tours are brief and free, but funnel visitors into huge showrooms where salespeople apply heavy sales pressure. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Demos" — Factory demonstrations are free; showroom pottery items are marked with elevated retail prices that require hard bargaining. (caution)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Genuine Artisans" — Working cooperative master potters and zellige tile carvers hand-chiseling authentic mosaic pieces from local clay. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Uneven Terrain" — Workshop floors are dusty, cracked, and punctuated by shallow pits and kiln steps; sturdy closed shoes needed. (caution)
### 🌿 Wellness & Living Arts (3)
### 23. Pottery & Mosaic Workshop at Art Naji (id: `fes-art-naji`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Kiln Hazards" — Working production facility with hot kilns, flying ceramic shards, and clay dust; closed-toe shoes recommended. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Commission Markup" — Tour guides steer groups here for hefty commissions; sticker prices in the sales hall are heavily inflated to compensate. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Bargaining Expected" — Factory demonstrations are free; showroom ceramics and zellige tables carry high price tags that require firm negotiation. (caution)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Master Craftsmanship" — Founded in 1930; genuine maâlems practice centuries-old hand-wheel throwing and hand-chiseling mosaic zellige. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Level Ground" — Ground-level facility with wide display corridors and minimal steps; largely accessible. (good)

---

### 24. Hammam Mernissi & Spa (id: `fes-hammam-mernissi`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Slippery Tile" — Wet marble and tiled floors are slick; hot steam rooms require using provided rubber sandals to prevent falls. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Upfront Confirmation" — Confirm package details (scrub, mask, massage length) at reception beforehand to prevent surprise add-ons at checkout. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Tiered Packages" — Spa menu packages clearly outline pricing (typically 250–600 MAD depending on scrub duration and massage options). (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Traditional Ritual" — Historic 16th-century bathhouse adapted for private/semi-private guest treatments using traditional black soap and kessa mitts. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Heat Demands" — Requires tolerating high steam heat, lying on heated stone slabs, and undergoing rigorous, firm full-body exfoliating scrubs. (caution)

---

### 25. The Ruined Garden (id: `fes-ruined-garden`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Calm Sanctuary" — Secure, enclosed walled garden setting inside Riad Idrissy, fully isolated from medina street traffic. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Alley Misdirection" — Touts in nearby Sidi Ahmed Chaoui alleys often claim the venue is closed; ignore them and follow mapped signage. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Transparent Menu" — Clearly printed menus with set prices (tapas-style plates 50–90 MAD, mains 120–180 MAD; specialty mechoui pre-ordered). (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Heritage Dining" — Celebrated for authentic regional Fassi recipes, slow-roasted mechoui, and Sephardic Moroccan dishes in a restored ruined riad. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Cobbled Approach" — Reaching the entrance requires walking narrow medina alleys; the internal garden seating is flat with only minor threshold steps. (good)
## Ifrane & Azrou — 10 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/ifrane_azrou/` — 6 heritage · 4 nature

### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (6)
### 1. Lion de Pierre (Ifrane Stone Lion) (id: `ifrane-lion-statue`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Public Park" — Located in a quiet, pedestrian-friendly open municipal garden with well-maintained paths and lighting. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Photo Touts" — Unofficial photographers and animal handlers (ponies/Barbary macaques) may hustle for paid photos; decline politely if uninterested. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Public outdoor sculpture; viewing and taking personal photos is completely free of charge. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Colonial Landmark" — Carved in 1930 directly into local limestone (attributed to French sculptor Henri Moreau) to honor the extinct Atlas lion. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Wheelchair Flat" — Completely level paved walkways through the central park with zero stairs; fully accessible for all ages. (good)

---

### 2. Centre d'Interprétation du Patrimoine du Moyen Atlas (CIPMA) (id: `azrou-cip-moyen-atlas`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Modern Facility" — Housed within the Azrou Cultural Center; clean, secure indoor facility with reception attendants. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Government Run" — Official cultural interpretation center operated under municipal and regional heritage authorities with no pushy vendors. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Entry" — Public cultural institution offering free (or nominal municipal) admission to visitors exploring regional heritage. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Curated Heritage" — Rigorous exhibitions across three pavilions dedicated to Middle Atlas natural ecosystems, Amazigh customs, and regional archaeology. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Easy Indoors" — Smooth indoor flooring, wide doorways, and gentle ramps suitable for all mobility levels. (good)

---

### 3. Ensemble Artisanal d'Azrou (id: `azrou-ensemble-artisanal`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Peaceful Complex" — Organized state crafts complex with tranquil courtyards and dedicated artisan workshops. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Zero Touting" — State-sponsored cooperative environment with no aggressive touts or middleman commissions. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Clear, fixed prices set directly by the artisans and cooperatives without the need for stressful haggling. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Certified Cooperatives" — Authentic local craftspeople creating hand-knotted Beni M’Guild carpets, cedarwood carvings, and regional leather goods on site. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Level Ground" — Single-story layout with wide, flat corridors and minimal entry steps. (good)

---

### 4. Le Rocher d'Azrou (Akchmir) (id: `azrou-akchmir-rock`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Unrailed Edges" — The rocky crest and upper viewing platforms have steep drop-offs with limited or broken guardrails; exercise caution near the edges. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Low Risk" — Quiet natural landmark; occasional informal guides or children offering directions for small change. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Natural geological outcrop freely accessible to the public at all hours. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Geological Symbol" — The iconic calcareous-dolomitic rock formation that gave Azrou its Berber name (*Azrou* = "the rock"). (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Steep Steps" — Reaching the top viewpoint requires ascending a steep, uneven stone staircase with dozens of steps. (caution)

---

### 5. Grande Mosquée Ennour (id: `azrou-grande-mosquee-ennour`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Orderly Surroundings" — Central, well-lit urban area adjacent to public gardens and the weekly souk perimeter. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Respectful Area" — No high-pressure touts or souvenir scams around the perimeter; standard respectful etiquette applies. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Exterior" — No charge to admire the exterior architecture and courtyard gates; interior is restricted to Muslim worshippers. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Traditional Architecture" — Active spiritual hub featuring classic green-tiled roofs, intricate Atlas cedar accents, and local craftsmanship. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Surrounds" — Flat paved streets and open squares surrounding the mosque with easy curb ramps. (good)

---

### 6. Église Notre-Dame des Cèdres (id: `ifrane-notre-dame-des-cedres`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Calm Grounds" — Quiet, secluded parish setting shaded by historic Atlas cedar trees; very safe and tranquil. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Touts Free" — Wholly non-commercial religious site free of touts, guides, or souvenir sellers. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Entry" — Free admission for worship and quiet visiting during open hours; voluntary donations welcome. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Alpine Modernist" — Historic 1939 Catholic church designed by French architect Paul Tournon, blending regional cedar timberwork with alpine stone. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Gently Sloped" — Approach involves flat walkways and slight lawn inclines with a few low stone entry steps. (good)

### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (4)
### 7. Cèdre Gouraud Forest & Barbary Macaques (id: `azrou-cedre-gouraud-forest`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Primate Caution" — Macaques are wild animals and can scratch or bite if cornered, teased, or handed food; keep a respectful distance. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Peanut Hustle" — Persistent vendors push overpriced bags of peanuts (10–30 MAD) and monkey photo setups; feeding is ecologically harmful. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Free entry into the national park forest; informal parking attendant fee of 10–20 MAD. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Ancient Cedars" — High-altitude Middle Atlas cedar biosphere harboring endangered wild Barbary macaques and the famous 800-year-old Gouraud cedar trunk. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Uneven Dirt" — Dirt forest trails with exposed tree roots and stones; gentle walking around the main clearing, mild slopes further out. (good)

---

### 8. Ain Vittel Spring & Cascades (id: `ifrane-ain-vittel`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Slippery Stream" — Riverside rocks and earthen paths can become slick with mud and water spray near the small cascades. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Pony Pressure" — Horse and pony handlers solicit visitors persistently; agree strictly on the duration and total cost before mounting. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Nature" — Access to the natural spring and walking paths along the stream is free; informal parking fee is 10–20 MAD. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Popular Picnic" — Genuine local family recreation spot shaded by maple and cedar woodlands, though heavily commercialized on peak weekends. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Gentle Stroll" — Flat dirt and stone riverside paths suitable for all fitness levels; minimal elevation change. (good)

---

### 9. Michlifen Ski Resort & Volcanic Crater (id: `ifrane-michlifen-ski-resort`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Dated Gear" — Ski infrastructure and rental gear can be basic and aged; snowy or icy slopes demand solid traction footwear. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Rental Haggling" — Roadside operators aggressively push sled rentals (50–100 MAD) and horse rides with fluctuating unmetered prices; negotiate beforehand. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Crater" — Visiting the volcanic caldera is free; lift passes (~100–220 MAD) and winter rentals operate with variable seasonal rates. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Volcanic Basin" — Historic 1950s ski center and extinct volcanic caldera (Grand Cratère) perched between 1,800 m and 2,000 m elevation. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Moderate Slopes" — Flat ground around the base and parking area, but walking up the crater rim or snowy ski slopes requires moderate physical effort. (caution)

---

### 10. Dayet Aoua Mountain Lake (id: `ifrane-dayet-aoua-lake`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Open Basin" — Broad natural perimeter with no safety barriers; surface transitions between loose gravel, dry dust, and marshy mud. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Ride Operators" — Horse and quad handlers gather along the shore and can be pushy; confirm route and total price firmly before riding. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Access" — Completely free public lake access; informal car parking attendants charge 10–20 MAD. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Weather Dependent" — A scenic natural mountain basin, though recurrent droughts frequently leave the lake bed completely dry. (caution)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Perimeter" — Level walking path around the lake shore and across the flat bed; easily walkable for strollers and all ages. (good)

## Marrakech — 15 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/marrakech/` — 13 heritage · 1 nature · 1 wellness

### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (13)
### 1. Bahia Palace (id: `marrakech-bahia-palace`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Well Managed" — Broad open courtyards with on-site security attendants and clearly marked one-way walking routes. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Gate Touts" — Unofficial guides gather outside claiming the palace is closed or offering overpriced private tours; buy only at the ticket booth. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Official admission is 100 MAD for foreign adults (30 MAD for Moroccan citizens/residents) at the counter. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Alawite Grandeur" — Genuine late 19th-century grand vizier palace featuring master-level zellige tilework, painted cedar ceilings, and leafy riads. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Mostly Level" — Ground-floor complex with flat marble and tiled courtyards; some low marble door saddles and sunken garden edges. (good)

---

### 2. Ben Youssef Madrasa (id: `marrakech-ben-youssef-madrasa`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Restored Structure" — Extensively restored by the Ministry of Habous; well-lit courtyards with regulated visitor entry flow. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Direct Ticketing" — Official ticket counter prevents tout pricing; ignore street youths claiming the madrasa is closed for prayers. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Admission is 50 MAD per foreign adult (20 MAD for Moroccan residents) purchased at the entrance booth. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Saadian Treasure" — 16th-century Islamic college showcasing historic stuccowork, cedar latticework, and 130 former student dormitories. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Narrow Stairs" — Main courtyard and prayer hall are flat; reaching upper dormitories requires climbing narrow, winding stone stairs. (caution)

---

### 3. Jemaa el-Fnaa (id: `marrakech-jemaa-el-fnaa`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Pickpocket Risk" — Dense, chaotic crowds day and night; keep valuables secured and watch out for speeding scooters crossing the square. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Aggressive Performers" — Snake charmers, monkey handlers, and henna artists force interactions and demand excessive tips (100–300 MAD) for photos. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Menu Clarification" — Food stalls have printed menus, but clarify exact portion prices and decline unordered bread/olives to avoid padded bills. (caution)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Oral Masterpiece" — UNESCO-recognized intangible cultural space where authentic Berber musicians, storytellers, and acrobats still gather. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Walkable" — Broad, flat open asphalt and paved pedestrian expanse; zero stairs, though navigating thick crowds requires patience. (good)

---

### 4. Saadian Tombs (id: `marrakech-saadian-tombs`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Narrow Queues" — Compact walled complex; long, tight queues form in the narrow courtyard to view the Hall of Twelve Pillars. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Official Entrance" — Entrance is regulated through an official ticket counter inside the secure Kasbah alleyway. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Official admission fee is 100 MAD for foreign adults (30 MAD for Moroccan residents) paid at the gate. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Untouched Saadian" — Sealed for centuries until rediscovery in 1917, preserving exquisite 16th-century Italian Carrara marble and gilded honeycomb stucco. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Flat Pathways" — Entire mausoleum garden and chambers are situated on a single flat ground level with accessible paved paths. (good)

---

### 5. Koutoubia Mosque (id: `marrakech-koutoubia-mosque`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Well Patrolled" — Spacious open grounds surrounded by municipal gardens with a constant police and municipal security presence. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "False Access" — Scammers near the gates offer paid "secret entry" to tourists; interior access is strictly reserved for Muslims only. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Exterior" — No charge to explore the exterior plaza, minaret gardens, and archaeological foundations. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Almohad Icon" — Landmark 12th-century Almohad minaret that established the architectural model for Seville's Giralda and Rabat's Hassan Tower. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Paved Gardens" — Completely flat paved plazas and crushed-gravel garden walkways with bench seating throughout. (good)

---

### 6. El Badi Palace (id: `marrakech-el-badi-palace`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Rampart Drop-Offs" — Massive open ruin with high ramparts; watch your step near sunken garden pits and unrailed wall sections. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Gate Touts" — Unofficial guides linger near the main entrance; buy tickets directly from the official ticket desk. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Admission is 100 MAD for foreign adults (30 MAD for Moroccan residents); covers palace grounds and underground vaults. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Monumental Ruin" — 16th-century Saadian palace ruins built by Ahmad al-Mansur, housing nesting storks and vast sunken orange tree groves. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Extensive Walking" — Large open-air area with dirt tracks, sun-exposed courtyards, and stairs leading down to subterranean storage chambers. (caution)

---

### 7. Maison de la Photographie (id: `marrakech-maison-de-la-photographie`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Private Museum" — Quiet, secure riad environment with dedicated reception and well-supervised exhibition halls. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Honest Cultural" — Professional museum space with clear pricing, bookstore receipts, and no commercial pressure. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Admission ticket is 50 MAD per person; roof terrace café offers beverages at clear, fixed menu prices. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Historical Archives" — Authentic vintage photography collection (1870–1960) documenting Morocco’s diverse ethnographic and regional history. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Multi-Level Stairs" — Exhibition spans three levels connected by traditional steep riad staircases with no lift access. (caution)

---

### 8. Dar Si Said Museum (id: `marrakech-dar-si-said-museum`)
- **Safety:** Tag "State Regulated" — Operated under the Fondation Nationale des Musées (FNM) with on-site curators and gallery guards. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Zero Hustle" — Clean ticketing process with official receipts; completely free of medina retail pressure. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Official admission is 30 MAD per adult (free admission for children and on select national days). (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Living Textile" — 19th-century palace showcasing Morocco’s authentic weaving traditions, tribal rugs, and historic bridal cedar domes. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Moderate Stairs" — Ground-floor riads and courtyards are flat, but reaching the ornate first-floor reception hall requires climbing stairs. (good)

---

### 9. Souk Semmarine (id: `marrakech-souk-semmarine`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Congested Thoroughfare" — Main medina bazaar packed with pedestrians, handcarts, and motorbikes; hold onto personal belongings. (caution)
- **Scams:** Tag "Aggressive Markups" — Vendors frequently quote tourist prices inflated by 300–500%; aggressive sales tactics and "Berber market today only" claims are common. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Heavy Haggling" — No fixed prices on carpets, lamps, or leather; rigorous bargaining is necessary, typically starting at 30–50% of initial quotes. (caution)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Commercial Mix" — A blend of genuine handcrafted Moroccan goods alongside imported souvenir knockoffs; inspect quality closely. (caution)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Packed Cobblestone" — Flat cobblestone walking under iron roof arches, but dense foot traffic and dodging motorbikes can be physically tiring. (good)

---

### 10. Dar El Bacha (Musée des Confluences) (id: `marrakech-dar-el-bacha`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Immaculate Complex" — Premier state-run museum under the FNM with attentive security guards and climate-controlled galleries. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Coffee Queue" — Bacha Coffee inside operates a separate waiting queue (often 1–3 hours); museum tickets do not guarantee a café seat. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Museum entry ticket is 60 MAD per adult; coffee salon charges upscale luxury prices with printed menus. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Pasha Palace" — Lavish 1910 palace of Pasha Thami El Glaoui, presenting authentic zellige, carved plaster, and interfaith Moroccan exhibitions. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Wheelchair Friendly" — Ground level is largely step-free, paved with smooth historic tiles, making it one of the most accessible riads. (good)

---

### 11. Le Jardin Secret (id: `marrakech-le-jardin-secret`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Enclosed Oasis" — Well-maintained private botanical garden with paved pathways, low water fountains, and attentive staff. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Official Operation" — Professional ticketing desk and orderly visitors' circuit; free of medina sales solicitations. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Admission is 100 MAD for adults (discounts for Moroccans and youth); optional panoramic tower access costs 40 MAD extra. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Sacred Geometry" — Reconstructed 19th-century riad palace showcasing authentic Islamic garden quadripartite design and historic water hydraulics. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Wheelchair Ramps" — Garden walkways are paved, wide, and equipped with ramps; only the panoramic tower requires climbing narrow stairs. (good)

---

### 12. Almoravid Koubba (Koubba Ba'adiyin) (id: `marrakech-almoravid-koubba`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Fenced Trench" — Excavated several meters below modern street level; stone steps and guardrails line the perimeter descent. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Low Risk" — Direct ticketing desk adjacent to the Ben Youssef plaza with zero tout interference inside the enclosure. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Official admission fee is 50 MAD paid at the front entrance booth. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Sole Almoravid" — The only surviving structure in Marrakech from the 12th-century Almoravid dynasty, featuring original ablution dome architecture. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Subterranean Steps" — Reaching the base of the monument requires descending and climbing a flight of stone steps into the excavation basin. (caution)

---

### 13. Musée Yves Saint Laurent Marrakech (id: `marrakech-yves-saint-laurent-museum`)
- **Safety:** Tag "World Class" — Modern, air-conditioned museum facility with professional security, bag checks, and managed visitor capacities. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Online Only" — Tickets must be purchased in advance online from the official portal; street scalpers outside sell invalid or inflated tickets. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Advance Booking" — Standalone museum ticket is ~135 MAD (combined tickets with Jardin Majorelle available); strictly enforced timeslots. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Fashion Heritage" — Dedicated exhibition honoring Yves Saint Laurent’s creative work, directly inspired by Moroccan textiles, colors, and culture. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Fully Accessible" — Modern, single-story architectural design with wide corridors, smooth floors, and full wheelchair accessibility. (good)
### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (1)
### 14. Cyber Parc Arsat Moulay Abdeslam (id: `marrakech-cyber-parc`)
- **Safety:** Tag "Enclosed Haven" — Walled municipal park with monitored gates and security staff; quiet, peaceful, and fully buffered from city traffic. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Zero Hustle" — Completely non-commercial public park; no touts, fake guides, or pushy vendors permitted on the grounds. (good)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Free Entry" — 100% free municipal botanical park and telecom exhibition space with zero admission fees. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Royal Orchard" — 18th-century princely estate (arsat) created by Prince Moulay Abdeslam, restored with heritage olive trees, citrus groves, and modern Wi-Fi kiosks. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Wheelchair Flat" — Wide, packed-dirt and paved red-sand paths on a single flat level with abundant shade and benches; universally accessible. (good)
### 🌿 Wellness & Living Arts (1)
### 15. Jardin Majorelle (id: `marrakech-jardin-majorelle`)
- **Safety:** Tag "High Security" — Mandatory bag screening at entry with security personnel and strictly managed visitor capacities along the paths. (good)
- **Scams:** Tag "Online Only" — No tickets are sold on site; avoid street scalpers outside selling invalid QR codes and purchase solely on the official portal. (caution)
- **Pricing:** Tag "Fixed Price" — Admission is 170 MAD for foreign adults (optional +60 MAD for the Pierre Bergé Museum of Berber Arts) with strictly timed slots. (good)
- **Authenticity:** Tag "Iconic Sanctuary" — Historic 1930s Art Deco artist estate and botanical collection, saved and preserved by Yves Saint Laurent and Pierre Bergé. (good)
- **Difficulty:** Tag "Level Walkways" — Smooth paved and packed gravel paths equipped with ramps; universally accessible for strollers, wheelchairs, and all ages. (good)


## Merzouga — 21 listings
> Files: `src/listings/things/merzouga/` — 6 heritage · 13 nature · 2 wellness
### 🏛️ Heritage & Culture (6)
### 1. Gnawa Music Performance at Khamlia Village (id: merzouga-gnawa-music)

- **Safety**: Tag "Very Safe" — Performances take place in secure, seated community music houses with welcoming local hospitality. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Reputable" — Managed by established local cultural associations (such as Association Bambara) rather than commercial commission touts. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Donation Based" — Admission and mint tea are free; visitors customarily leave a direct tip of 20–50 MAD per person or purchase group CDs. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Living Heritage" — UNESCO-recognized musical tradition practiced by direct descendants of sub-Saharan migrants preserving sacred healing trance rhythms. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Effortless" — Paved road access directly to the village with zero walking strain; seating is on ground carpets or low bench cushions. (good)

---

### 2. Prehistoric Rock Engravings of Taouz (id: merzouga-taouz-petroglyphs)

- **Safety**: Tag "Remote Track" — Located in exposed desert close to the Algerian border area; an experienced off-road driver or local escort is strongly recommended. (caution)
- **Scams**: Tag "Unregulated Guides" — No official ticket kiosk exists, leading occasional self-appointed village guides to demand arbitrary fees for pointing out rock faces. (caution)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Free Entry" — Natural open-air site on public desert land with no entrance tickets; a 50–100 MAD negotiated gratuity is typical for a village guide. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Ancient Art" — Millennia-old petroglyphs carved into black desert rock patina, depicting Saharan wild fauna, warriors, and prehistoric two-wheeled chariots. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Uneven Terrain" — Requires a 4x4 drive over dirt pistes followed by walking over loose desert stones, gravel, and unpaved low rocky ridges. (caution)

---

### 3. Historic M'Fis Desert Mines & Ghost Town (id: merzouga-mfis-mines)

- **Safety**: Tag "Hazardous Shafts" — Open-cast pits and vertical mine shafts descend tens of meters without safety rails; stay back from unstable crumbling trench edges. (caution)
- **Scams**: Tag "Ungoverned Site" — Free from commercial vendors and ticketing touts, though informal sellers occasionally offer local barite, galena, or quartz stones. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Free Entry" — Completely ungated with zero entry charges or formal toll gates; modest tips are customary only if an artisan miner shows you around. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Untouched Ruin" — Genuine 20th-century French colonial lead and kohl mining village, preserved in an un-staged, deteriorating state atop the desert plateau. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Rough Ground" — Reached via bumpy off-road desert tracks, followed by short walks over loose shale, rocks, and crumbling building debris. (caution)

---

### 4. Hassi Labied Oasis & Traditional Khettara Walk (id: merzouga-hassi-labied-oasis)

- **Safety**: Tag "Very Safe" — Gentle community gardens right next to village guesthouses; clean shaded paths with zero exposure hazards. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Hassle Free" — Working agricultural cooperative managed peacefully by local families; no aggressive touting or forced shopping traps. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Free Access" — Completely free to stroll on your own; private guided tours from village residents cost an optional nominal fee (approx. 50–100 MAD). (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Working Oasis" — Fully functional farming plots utilizing centuries-old subterranean *khettara* gravity-fed irrigation channels to nurture date palms and crops. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Easy Stroll" — Level, packed-dirt trails under shaded palm trees, requiring minimal physical stamina and suitable for all ages. (good)

---

### 5. Morocco National 4x4 Auto Museum (id: merzouga-auto-museum)

- **Safety**: Tag "Well Maintained" — Enclosed, flat hangar exhibition space directly accessible off the paved N13 highway with secure parking. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Unofficial Fees" — The museum is often promoted as free, but site caretakers commonly request informal entry fees or tips at the door. (caution)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Tip Expected" — Official access is free or nominal, but visitors should anticipate giving 10–20 MAD per person directly to the on-site guard. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Private Collection" — Genuine display of roughly 30 desert-adapted vehicles belonging to Emirati Sheikh Hamad bin Hamdan, including modified Jeeps and expedition relics. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Level Floor" — Compact, single-floor indoor facility on flat ground; effortless to tour in 20 to 30 minutes. (good)

---

### 6. Old Ksar Merzouga Earthen Village Walk (id: merzouga-ksar-heritage)

- **Safety**: Tag "Crumbling Structures" — Safe during daylight hours, though unlit passages and crumbling mudbrick lintels require care when stepping near abandoned ruins. (caution)
- **Scams**: Tag "Hassle Free" — Quiet residential quarter largely free from aggressive tourist guides or high-pressure curio sellers. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Completely Free" — Open public village with zero admission tickets or gated access checkpoints. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Living Settlement" — Real Berber pre-Saharan earthen architecture constructed from traditional sun-dried clay, straw, and palm beams, still housing local families. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Flat Walk" — Completely level stroll along sand and dirt village lanes; no hills or demanding climbs involved. (good)

### 🏜️ Nature & Adventure (13)

### 7. Camel Trekking to Sunset in Erg Chebbi (id: merzouga-camel-trek)

- **Safety**: Tag "Tethered Caravan" — Camels walk in a single-file caravan led on foot by an experienced camelier; sturdy padded saddles with front handles are provided. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Vetted Itinerary" — Book through established riads or licensed desert guides; ensure sunset dune stops and luggage handling are included in writing. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Fixed Rate" — Standard sunset rides cost 150–250 MAD (~€15–€25) per person; a 20–50 MAD tip for the camel handler is customary. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Nomadic Tradition" — Genuine centuries-old mode of desert transport across Erg Chebbi, maintained by local Amazigh (Berber) handlers. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Hip Strain" — Low aerobic effort, but mounting, dismounting, and the wide rhythmic saddle sway can strain knees, hips, and lower back muscles. (caution)

---

### 8. Quad Biking & ATV Dune Adventure (id: merzouga-quad-adventure)

- **Safety**: Tag "Protective Gear" — Helmets and goggles are mandatory, and rides follow an escort guide; rollover risks exist on steep dune crests if driven too fast. (caution)
- **Scams**: Tag "Licensed Base" — Book at reputable ATV centers with established vehicle garages; inspect brake and tire conditions before signing waivers. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Hourly Rate" — Clear rates range from 400–600 MAD (~€40–€60) per hour for a single quad, including fuel, safety gear, and guide accompaniment. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Modern Adrenaline" — Modern motorized recreational sport designed for speed and dune thrills rather than traditional Saharan cultural immersion. (caution)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Arm Fatigue" — Requires solid upper-body control, throttle grip endurance, and active balance to steer through shifting, soft desert sands. (caution)

---

### 9. Overnight Glamping in a Sahara Desert Camp (id: merzouga-glamping)

- **Safety**: Tag "Fully Guarded" — Camps feature round-the-clock on-site staff, enclosed heavy-canvas tents, private en-suite flush bathrooms, and hot running water. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Verified Tier" — Service levels vary widely between basic bivouacs and true luxury camps; inspect verified traveler photos to ensure private facilities match claims. (caution)
- **Pricing**: Tag "All Inclusive" — Established luxury camps cost 800–2,500 MAD (~€80–€250) per tent per night, covering dinner, breakfast, campfire music, and 4x4 transfers. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Berber Hospitality" — Traditional wool/camel-hair tent aesthetics blended with modern comforts, featuring genuine live drumming and singing around the night fire. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Effortless" — Zero hiking required as 4x4 vehicles transport travelers and baggage straight to camp; suitable for all age groups and physical abilities. (good)

---

### 10. Birdwatching at Dayet Srji Salt Lake (id: merzouga-dayet-srji)

- **Safety**: Tag "Boggy Mud" — Silt along the shoreline can be soft and marshy; stay on firm sandy ground to avoid getting shoes or vehicles stuck. (caution)
- **Scams**: Tag "Open Access" — Natural public wetlands with zero admission checkpoints or official entry fees; ignore anyone attempting to sell access tickets. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Free Entry" — 100% free to explore on your own; optional local bird guides or 4x4 transfers charge modest, negotiable fees. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Seasonal Wetland" — Genuine desert wetland stopover for migratory greater flamingos, shelducks, and desert larks; note the lake is completely dry in hot summer months. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Flat Walking" — Completely flat terrain along sandy shores; bring water and brimmed hats as there is no shade or natural cover. (good)

---

### 11. Exploring the French-era Mines of Mifis (id: merzouga-mifis-mines)

- **Safety**: Tag "Unsecured Shafts" — Deep vertical mine shafts and open trench edges have no railings; maintain a safe distance from deteriorating ledge rims. (caution)
- **Scams**: Tag "Informal Sellers" — The site is unticketed, but independent mineral vendors approach visitors selling local quartz, galena, or barite specimens at negotiable prices. (caution)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Free Entry" — No state admission fee or gate; offering a 20–50 MAD gratuity is polite if an on-site local miner shows you around the pits. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Colonial Relic" — Real 1950s French colonial lead and kohl extraction works, left in an unvarnished state surrounded by abandoned miner quarters. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Stony Terrain" — Involves driving rough off-road pistes followed by walking over loose gravel, scree, and rubble on an exposed plateau. (caution)

---

### 12. Sandboarding on Erg Chebbi Dunes (id: merzouga-sandboarding)

- **Safety**: Tag "Soft Landing" — Soft desert sand cushions falls, but fast descents can cause tumbling; wear sunglasses and a scarf to shield eyes and mouth from blowing sand. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Gear Check" — Desert camps and street rental shops frequently provide worn snowboards with missing straps; verify base wax and binding straps before hiking up. (caution)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Camp Included" — Most overnight camps supply boards free of charge; independent rental from Merzouga shops costs 50–100 MAD (~€5–€10) per board. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Modern Sport" — Modern recreational sand sport adapted from snowboarding for Saharan tourists rather than an indigenous heritage activity. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Exhausting Climb" — Riding down takes seconds with zero effort, but trudging back up 50-meter sand dunes in sinking sand is cardiovascularly exhausting. (caution)

---

### 13. Visiting a Traditional Nomad Family (id: merzouga-nomad-family)

- **Safety**: Tag "Very Safe" — Hospitable family settlements across the flat desert hamada; follow your guide's lead and ask permission before photographing family members. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Vetted Guide" — Some mass-tour routes stop at pseudo-nomad tents set up purely for tips; ensure your guide takes you to genuine pastoralist families. (caution)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Modest Gratuity" — While mint tea is offered freely out of hospitality, visitors should leave a respectful tip of 50–100 MAD or purchase handmade woven crafts. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Pastoral Life" — Real semi-nomadic Amazigh families residing in woven camel-hair tents (*khaimas*), grazing goats and camels in the arid plains. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Accessible" — Reached by 4x4 directly to the camp with minimal flat walking; seating is on ground rugs inside low-clearance tents. (good)

---

### 14. Authentic Madfouna (Berber Pizza) (id: merzouga-madfouna)

- **Safety**: Tag "Freshly Baked" — Baked to order in intensely hot earthen ovens (*ferran*); hygienic, thoroughly cooked, and served steaming hot. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Bake-To-Order" — Avoid tourist cafes serving pre-cooked reheated wedges; visit dedicated local bakeries in Rissani or Merzouga where butcher-fresh fillings are prepared on the spot. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Per Kilo" — Transparently priced by pie size and meat weight, averaging 60–120 MAD (~€6–€12) for a large stuffed bread feeding 2 to 4 people. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Regional Specialty" — Quintessential Tafilalet culinary tradition: stuffed round flatbread packed with spiced beef, suet, boiled eggs, almonds, and desert herbs. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Effortless" — Traditional sit-down dining requiring zero physical exertion, other than waiting 30–45 minutes for fresh oven baking. (good)

---

### 15. Guided Stargazing & Desert Astronomy (id: merzouga-stargazing)

- **Safety**: Tag "Dark Surroundings" — Desert dunes are pitch black at night and temperatures drop sharply; carry a red-beam headlamp and bring warm jacket layers. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Confirmed Gear" — Confirm whether your package includes a high-powered computerized telescope and trained astronomy guide or just simple naked-eye campfire viewing. (caution)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Transparent Fee" — Casual naked-eye sky viewing is free at any desert camp; specialized telescope observation sessions cost 250–500 MAD (~€25–€50) per person. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Pristine Skies" — Certified Bortle Class 1–2 ultra-dark desert sky offering brilliant, unpolluted views of the Milky Way, meteor showers, and constellations. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Stationary" — Minimal physical effort sitting or standing near the camp perimeter; suitable for everyone, provided warm clothing is worn against night chills. (good)

---

### 16. Sahara Sand Bath Therapy (Psammotherapy) (id: merzouga-sand-bath)

- **Safety**: Tag "Medical Pre-check" — Practiced during peak summer midday heat (~45°C); strictly unadvised for anyone with heart conditions, hypertension, or asthma due to intense thermal strain. (caution)
- **Scams**: Tag "Trained Hosts" — Book solely through established village auberges whose practitioners monitor vital hydration and strictly enforce safe immersion durations (10–15 min max). (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Fixed Session" — Standard sessions cost 200–350 MAD (~€20–€35), covering sand excavation, sun umbrella shading, constant water service, and post-bath herbal tea recovery. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Ancestral Cure" — Ancient Amazigh therapeutic tradition utilizing the mineral-rich, solar-heated sands of Erg Chebbi to alleviate joint pain and rheumatism. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Heavy Strain" — Physically strenuous cardiovascular endurance test from extreme heat exposure and the heavy weight of sand pressing on the torso. (caution)

---

### 17. 4x4 Off-Road Desert Safari (id: merzouga-safari)

- **Safety**: Tag "Licensed Chauffeur" — Driven by experienced desert professionals in maintained 4WD vehicles stocked with spare tires, tow straps, and compressor pumps. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Set Circuit" — Agree on the complete circuit stops (dunes, M'Fis mines, nomad tents, Khamlia) beforehand to prevent drivers from skipping planned points. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Vehicle Rate" — Clearly priced by private vehicle (up to 4–6 passengers), typically 600–900 MAD (~€60–€90) for a comprehensive half-day tour. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Rally Trails" — Traverses authentic historic Paris-Dakar rally tracks, ancient fossil fields, and untouched black stone desert around Erg Chebbi. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Bumpy Ride" — Sitting inside the vehicle requires no stamina, but prolonged jarring over desert washboards and dune crests can provoke motion sickness. (caution)

---

### 18. Sunset Horseback Riding in the Sahara (id: merzouga-horse-riding)

- **Safety**: Tag "Trained Horses" — Well-conditioned Barb and Arab-Barb horses paired to rider experience levels; guided by mounted local equestrian handlers with basic helmets. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Vetted Stables" — Book through specialized local stables (such as Merzouga Horse Riding) to ensure properly sheltered mounts and punctual sunset departures. (good)
- **Pricing**: Tag "Hourly Rate" — Transparent hourly pricing, typically running 300–400 MAD (~€30–€40) per person for a 1-hour sunset ride into the sand dunes. (good)
- **Authenticity**: Tag "Equine Heritage" — Celebrates Morocco's deep equestrian culture, riding traditional desert horses through quiet perimeter sands away from motor noise. (good)
- **Difficulty**: Tag "Core Balance" — Requires active leg grip, posture balance, and core engagement, especially when horses climb or descend shifting dune slopes. (caution)

---

### 19. Tea and Art at Galerie Chez les Artistes (id: merzouga-chez-artistes)

- **Safety**: Tag "Very Safe" — Cozy, secure artist workshop and home gallery located in peaceful Ksar Khamlia with warm family hospitality. (good)
- **Scams**: Tag "Pressure Free" — Operated independently by the resident painter couple Johanna and Lahcen Mahmoudi; no tout commissions or aggressive sales pitches. (good)
